Global Financial Markets                                                 梫杶ꄌ踅䄬㕙
The Group’s global financial markets segment provides                    儗꥘㎯氳梫杶ꄌ踅䄬㕙⮇鼨䬠❠㎜꤀祺
international brokerage, wealth management and asset                     硕靸 㳫 畚 杼 ⹖ 鞳 榣 畚 杼 僜 ⳉ ◀  杶 氳 㲙
management services to clients worldwide.                                䢒

The brokerage division offers a full suite of financial instruments     祺硕鼨ꝛ憡⪭냖屻⡭㲙䢒䬠❠亡⛤氳ꄌ
and investment products to its high net worth clients, spanning         踅 䄄  ⹖ 䤸 鞳 榣 ⿁ⴎ 䦮 亦 꺞 ╈ ㎜ ⪐ ㏑
securities, futures and options traded on exchanges in Hong Kong,        鵂 鷪 帽  鵡 ⹖ 岒  鵡 銷 ⱁ ⹖ ╚ 釒 宿 㛛
the Chinese Mainland (via Shanghai and Shenzhen-Hong Kong               ㎜ 㳄 ◬ 佄 䢦 鞙 韢 ╯ 镜 ⯉僿 鞁 ⹖ 僿 垷♓ ⹖
Stock Connect schemes) and major overseas countries, as well            鏂 罇 垷 镜⥍ ⯉㓹 ꄌ 䤸 鞳 ⹖ 㰃 㷤 踅 鞳儗
as warrants, bonds, fund investments and margin financing. The          ꥘㎯氳私┕⹖䣆嚂◬佄䇖⺐✳⪭㲙䢒鶡车
Group’s web-based and mobile trading platforms enable real-time         㴨 侇 ◬ 佄 ⹖ 泃 䫞 䤸 鞳 磞 ⺭
transaction and investment portfolio monitoring for its clients.

The wealth management division offers balanced solutions,                靸 㳫 畚 杼 鼨 ꝛ 䬠 ❠ 䇖 辆 氳 鉮 婋 亡 勈⶜ Ⲃ
assisting high net worth clients in constructing customised and          ⪭냖屻⡭㲙䢒䋧环ꄈ骰㲋⯆┦꭛䓪氳䤸
resilient investment portfolios comprising a diverse range of            鞳 磞 ⺭ⴎ 䦮 ⟊ ꤠ㓹 ꄌ⥍ ⯉ ⹖ 罇 烨 璣 㛡
financial products, including insurance, funds, bonds and stocks.        嘚 ⴫ ꄌ 踅 榣 ⿁

The asset management division offers tailored investment                 鞳 榣 畚 杼 鼨 ꝛ 䬠 ❠ ꄈ 骰 㲋 ⯆ 氳 䤸 鞳 僜 ⳉ
services, ranging from private investment funds, personalised            尝 衏 熗 Ⳕ 䤸 鞳 㓹 ꄌ⠕ ☕ ⴫  垷 㡨 鋖 磞 ⺭
discretionary portfolio management to investment advisory                畚 杼 ⹖ 䤸 鞳 钎 鍪 僜 ⳉ⶜ Ⲃ 㲙 䢒 暢 䐃 犹 㲋
services, helping its clients to generate stable and substantial         ┦ ⺎ 鈶 氳 ㍑ 㕕⪭ ◱ 畚 杼 ꝧ ䷒ 䌋 㓹 ꄌ 㑔 ⪝
returns. It also manages Open-Ended Fund Companies (OFC)                 ⺚ ( O F C ) ⹖ 僗 ꡿ ⺭ 㛸 㓹 ꄌ ( L P F )♓ ⹖ 䧏 僗
and Limited Partnership Funds (LPF), and holds Qualified Foreign         ⺭劲㗟㛛嚂啶䤸鞳縖(QFII)⹖⺭劲㗟㛛僗
Institutional Investor (QFII) and Qualified Foreign Limited Partner      ꡿ ⺭ 㛸 ☕ ( Q F L P ) 骰 ♷
(QFLP) statuses.

opportunities for advisory and customised discretionary                  氳  垷 㡨 鋖 䤸 鞳 僜 ⳉ 䬠 ❠ 륛 㛻 嚂 鷖㶩 塝
investment services. In this regard, the Group has stepped up            縡 銨儗 ꥘ ㎯ 䝬 課 ⪭ ◅ ⶇ 䇗 祺 꿨ⱶ 㛻 ⱱ 䈲
efforts and strived to capture the opportunities, drawing on its         ┾ 舜 ⱱ 䤧 䭃 嚂 鷖♓ 䬠 냖 梫 杶 ꄌ 踅 䄬 㕙 ⮇
two decades of experience, with an aim to increase the revenue           鼨 ╯ ䷅ ⪌
of the global financial markets segment.

The acquisition of Crosby Asia Limited (“Crosby”) was completed          C ro s by A s i a L i m i t e dրC ro s byց氳 ䷅ 頇

and Defaulted Loans                                                    ⥍ⳉ车Ⳃ
The Group has debt recovery procedures in place. For any loans         儗 ꥘ ㎯ 䄖 錄 环 ⥍ ⳉ 鴓 鋁  䈌㵴 亦 ♴ ⛰ ┘
with shortfall and/or overdue payments, demand letters and legal       餉 ꄌ 갯 ⹖ ҧ 䡝 鷀 僿 ♀ 堑 氳 鞚 堑㵩 氪 ⭴ ⤺
letters will be issued. If the borrower does not respond, the Group    堑 ⭺ ⹖ 嫏 䏱 ⭺ ♭⠭ ⠺ 堑 ☕ ┘ ⛼ ⭴ ㍑ 䟕儗
will engage external legal advisors for legal actions. Meanwhile,      ꥘ ㎯ 㵩 㡨 纷 㛛 鼨 嫏 䏱 걙 ゖ 䫑 ⹦ 嫏 䏱 车 Ⳃ
the Group will contact the borrower for additional collateral          ⺱ 侇儗 ꥘ ㎯ 㵩 绤 答 ⠺ 堑 ☕ ♓ 暢 ⹦ 갯 㛛 䥡
and/or settlement plan. The Group may engage debt collection           䥨 ⿁ ⹖ ҧ 䡝 り 鋁 磶 岳 銷 ⱁ㏇ 鸸 樧 䗯 媲 ┖
agents for such loan where appropriate. If the negotiation is not      儗 ꥘ ㎯ 㶩 僗 ꟯ 鞚 堑 ⺎ 耉 㡨 纷 ䷅ ⥍ ♏ 杼⠭
successful, or additional collateral is not sufficient or default in   儔 耉 鷾 䡗 ⶜ り䡝 갯 㛛 䥡 䥨 ⿁ ┘ 餉 䡝 磶 岳
settlement plan, external legal advisers will issue final warning      銷 ⱁ 鹂 ⹨ 鸂 硝㛛 鼨 嫏 䏱 걙 ゖ 㵩 ⺸ ⠺ 堑 ☕
to the borrower. Subsequently, writs of summon will be served to       氪 ⭴ 僅 䏳 閝 ⼔⪭ 䏳 㵩 ⺸ ⠺ 堑 ☕ 鴞 鷾 ⥉ 銻
the borrower to take proceedings to court.                             ♒ 敻♓ ⺸ 嫏 ꢚ 䬠  錐 鋧

 The Nomination Policy aims at assisting the Nomination            䬠⺲䷓瓁伀㏇⶜Ⲃ䬠⺲㡨⿦僎劯䴆蝕
 Committee in identifying and nominating suitable candidates       ◄僎㛡⩕⴫䷓瓁擻荈⹖䬠⺲⺭鸸氳蝕
 for directorship based on the Board Diversity Policy and sets     ◄ ⠰ 鹢 ☕┾ 鬼 ⮛ 僗 ꟯ ( i ) 䬠 ⺲ 亏 蝕 ◄
 out the nomination criteria and the nomination procedures         㖶 適 蝕 ◄ 僎 氳 舃 侇 狞 籭( i i ) 㶩 䋧 閲 ㏇
 for (i) nominating new Director to fill a casual vacancy on       罇典㛻僎┕鹢艈䡝ꄆ鹢氳♴⛰蝕◄⺸
 the Board; (ii) making recommendation to Directors and            蝕 ◄ ⹖ 罇 典 䬠 ⭴ 䋧 閲⹖ ( i i i ) 榺 罇 典
 Shareholders regarding any Director proposed for election         䬠⺲亏蝕◄⹉鹢氳䬠⺲嘅嶗⹖䬠⺲
 or re-election at general meeting; and (iii) nomination by        䈌
 Shareholders on election of new Director.

Risk Management and Internal Control                                  겨ꤠ畚杼⹖⪐鼨泃䫞
A. Goals and Objectives                                               A. 㲄伀⹖泘嘅
    Internal control is fundamental to the successful operation          ⪐鼨泃䫞╤䡗Ⱶ揩鷠⹖仼䅻哅ⳉ鷠⛼
    and day-to-day running of a business and it assists a company        ╯ 㓹 灅僗 Ⲃ ⪝ ⺚ 鷾 䡗 ⪭ 哅 ⳉ 泘 嘅鸸
    in achieving its business objectives. The appropriate and            樧⹖僗䷞氳겨ꤠ泃䫞硎礵伀㏇畚杼縡
    effective Risk Control Systems are designed to manage rather         ꪞ 寕 ꢝ 儔 耉 鷾 䡗 哅 ⳉ 泘 嘅 ╯ 겨 ꤠ┾
    than eliminate risks of failure to achieve business objectives,      ⥪⺎㶩ꄆ㛻ꎭ鏲ꢷ鳯䡝䮇㜊䬠❠⺭杼
    and can only provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance         縡 ꪞ 磿 㵴 ⟊ 镜
    against material misstatement or loss.

C. Risk Management                                                 C. 겨ꤠ畚杼
  (1) Risk Management Process                                       (1) 겨ꤠ畚杼䈌
      The risk management process includes risk identification,         겨 ꤠ 畚 杼  䈌 ⴎ 䦮 겨 ꤠ 閂 ⮯겨
      risk evaluation, risk management measures and risk                ꤠ 鍑 ⚽겨 ꤠ 畚 杼 䫣 亩 ⹖ 겨 ꤠ 泃
      control and review.                                               䫞 ⹖ 囸 鋁

      The management is entrusted with duties to identify,             畚杼㷼暢㡨孭亦⪭鞊♴⹖垷ⱱ疘
      analyse, evaluate, respond, monitor and communicate              ㎠ ⪐ 閂 ⮯⮇ 冫鍑 ⚽䟕 㵴泃
      risks associated with any activity, function or process          㴕 ⹖ ⥉ 鷾 艃 ♴ ⛰ 孨 Ⳃ绹 耉 䡝 
      within its scope of responsibility and authority. Risks          䈌 僗 ꟯ 氳 겨 ꤠ蝕 ◄ 僎 ⹖ 畚 杼 㷼
      are evaluated by the Board and management based on               劯䴆(i)겨ꤠ㵴儗꥘㎯靸ⳉ哅端⹖
      (i) the severity of the impact of the risks on the Group’s       揩 鷠 鵴 䡗 䏆 ꮷ ╯ ㌅ ꄆ 䓪⹖ ( i i ) 겨
      financial results and operations; and (ii) the probability       ꤠ 氪 榟 ╯ 哯 曎 ⹖ 갓 曎㵴 겨 ꤠ 鶡
      and frequency that the risks will occur.                         车 鍑 ⚽

      Based on the risk evaluation, the Company will manage            劯䴆겨ꤠ鍑⚽儗⪝⺚㵩䧞♓┖亡
      the risks through the following approach:                        䌋 畚 杼 겨 ꤠ

      •   Risk elimination – the management may identify               •   겨ꤠ寕ꢝ畚杼㷼⺎閂⮯겨
          the risks and implement certain changes or controls              ꤠ ⹖ 㴨 亩 萵 䇕 闭 Ⳃ 䡝 泃 䫞㱮
          that in effect eliminate the risk entirely.                       䪶 ꢝ 겨 ꤠ

      •   Risk mitigation – the management may implement               •   峎⛧겨ꤠ姢䇖畚杼㷼⺎㴨
          risk mitigation plan designed to reduce the likelihood           亩 겨 ꤠ 稸 鉮 銷 ⱁ伀 ㏇ ꡺ ⛧ 겨
          and impact of the risk to an acceptable level or                 ꤠ氪榟╯⺎耉䓪⹖䏆ꮷ舘⺎
          contingency plan for possible loss scenarios.                    䫚 ⹨ 姢 䇖䡝 㶩 弌 ㏇ 谱 䮇 䗯 㗟
                                                                           邯 㲋 䟕 闭 亡 勈

      •   Risk control and monitoring – it involves making             •   겨ꤠ泃䫞⹖泃㴕寘⹖㵴⺎
          decisions regarding which risks are acceptable and               䫚硱겨ꤠ⹖㞞⛰䟕㵴┘⺎䫚
          how to address those that are not. Accidents and                 ⹨ ╯ 겨 ꤠ ⛼ ⭴ 婋 㲋⛼ 憡 畚 杼
          other situations involving loss or near-loss will be             겨 ꤠ ╯ ┉ 鼨 ⮇㵴 寘 ⹖ 鵴 䡗 䮇
          investigated and properly documented as part of the              㜊䡝䫚鲥䮇㜊╯◄䷚⹖⪭☾
          effort to manage risks.                                          䗯 媲 㵩 鶡 车 鐪 刨 ┾ 㟱 憡 㰇 四

      •   Risk retention – the management may decide                   •   禵䧏겨ꤠ姢䇖畚杼㷼⺎婋
          that the risk rating is low enough that the risk is              㲋㓹亦겨ꤠ鍑碍㸋亦⛧舘⺎
          acceptable level and no action is required and the               䫚⹨姢䇖겨ꤠ縡夯ꯄ䫑⹦♴
          risk will continue to be monitored as part of the risk           ⛰ 䫣 亩♓ ⹖ ⛼ 憡 겨 ꤠ 畚 杼 銷
          management program to ensure the level of risk                   ⱁ 氳 ┉ 鼨 ⮇㵩 筷 箜 泃 㴕 겨 ꤠ
          does not increase to an unacceptable level.                      ♓澭⟊겨ꤠ┘僎┕ⶍ舘┘⺎
                                                                           䫚 ⹨ 姢 䇖

   (ii) Equity Risk                                               (ii) 垷沠겨ꤠ
        Equity risk arises from fluctuation in the price and           垷 沠 겨 ꤠ 榣 榟 舅 垷 沠❆ 㞞 罇
        volatility of equities such as stocks, equity portfolio        烨罇 烨 磞 ⺭ ⹖ 罇 䧘 僿 鞁 ╯
        and stock index futures. The Group does not have               ⧎ 劲 嫧 Ⳃ ⹖  ⚒儗 ꥘ ㎯ ┾ 扝
        proprietary equity investments. On the other hand,             㵬 僗 ╯ 垷 沠 䤸 鞳⺁ ┉ 亡 ꪫ
        the Group may be subject to exposure arising from              儗꥘㎯⺎耉䤖⹨ⴎꊷ䤖钱䢦
        the underwriting commitments. The Group mitigates              榣 榟 ╯ 겨 ꤠ儗 ꥘ ㎯ 鵂 鷪 㵴 氪
        its risks by conducting detailed analysis of the               车☕╯㓹儗ꪫ鶡车鎌碸⮇冫
        issuer’s fundamentals and pricing against market               ⹖㵴䄬㕙敻媲⹖ㄎ㞒鶡车㲋
        conditions and appetites. Such risk exposure is also           ⧎ ⮇ 冫♓ ꡺ ⛧ ⪭ 겨 ꤠ僗 ꟯
        reduced by way of internal sales and distribution and          겨ꤠ◱♓⪐鼨ꊷす⹖⮇ꊷ⹖
        by sub-underwritings.                                          ⮇ ⴎ ꊷ 亡 䌋 ◀ ♓ ꡺ ⛧

   (iii) Liquidity Risk                                           (iii) 孲Ⳃ鞳ꄌ겨ꤠ
         The Group’s financing business is a capital intensive          儗꥘㎯╯踅鞳哅ⳉ㸋鞳儗㳢

         Group is also exposed to liquidity risk arising                ┉ 鼨 ⮇儗 ꥘ ㎯ ◱ ꪫ 㵴 磶 畁 䢦
         from timing difference between settlement with                 䡝祺硕艃㲙䢒╯ꝴ磶畁侇䄐
         clearing houses or brokers and customers. The                  䢦 榣 榟 氳 孲 Ⳃ 鞳 ꄌ 겨 ꤠ孲 Ⳃ
         goal of liquidity risk management is to ensure the             鞳ꄌ겨ꤠ畚杼╯泘氳憡澭⟊

         business commitments as well as to comply with                 ♀ ⪭ 哅 ⳉ 䤖 䳸♓ ⹖ 鹘 㱥 鸸 榫
         the relevant Financial Resources Rules applying to             亦仝┖㛡㳄䧏擀ꡭ㸋⪝⺚╯
         various licensed subsidiaries.                                 注 ꟯ 靸 ⳉ 鞳 嶏 釨 ⯞

     (iv) Operational Risk                                             (iv) 揩鷠겨ꤠ

          by the Compliance Department and the Internal                     㴱 劭 鼨 ꝛ 鶡 车鎊 璣 鼨 ꝛ 㲋 僿

          management and the Audit Committee.

requisitionists and deposited at the principal place of business       哅 ㏑ 럅 錝 伺 ䷅ ♭ ☕ 憡 ⪝ ⺚ 熺 債    ┾ ⺎ 榺

order, the Company Secretary will ask the Board to convene a           罇 典 攑 ⮯ 㛻 僎⹛ ╯⠭ 僗 ꟯ 鑂 姲 祺 劭 㴨 憡

contrary, if the requisition has been verified as not in order, the    縡 罇 典 攑 ⮯ 㛻 僎 㵩 ┘ 僎 䟕 釒 姲 ⺉ ꝧ

    for forming an opinion on the Group financial statements.             ⳉ㕕辑䎬䡗䙬釤氳㓹灅䡘⠟靷鞊䧘㵷

    We remain solely responsible for our audit opinion.                   鞊♴

1. General                                                             1. ┉芙鞳乢
   Emperor Capital Group Limited (the “Company”) is                      葐永鞳儗꥘㎯僗꡿⪝⺚ր儗⪝⺚ց╤劯
   incorporated and registered as an exempted company with               䴆氬䛱鷾1981䇗⪝⺚嫏祺⟣銬錝⫈䡗

2. Changes in Accounting Policies (Continued)                       2. 僎銷䷓瓁闭Ⳃ箜
   New and amendments to HKFRS Accounting                              䄖ꯓ⛜⛙㶉儔榟䷞╯亏銬⹖祺
   Standards in issue but not yet effective                            ⟣銬꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞僎銷嶗
   (Continued)                                                         ⯞箜
   Amendments to HKFRS 9 and HKFRS 7 “Amendments to the              꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮9谝⹖꺞靸ⳉ㕕
   Classification and Measurement of Financial Instruments”          ⼔嶗⯞瑮7谝╯⟣銬儗րꄌ踅䄄╯⮇
                                                                     ⹖銷ꄈ╯⟣銬儗ց
   The amendments to HKFRS 9 clarify the recognition and             꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮9谝╯⟣銬儗ꄊ岳
   derecognition for financial asset and financial liability and     ꄌ踅鞳榣⹖ꄌ踅靷⥍╯澭鏂⹖䱨ꢝ澭
   add an exception which permits an entity to deem a financial      鏂┾ 㘎 ⱶ ┉ ꯁ ❆ 㛛 䗯 媲ⷊ 樧 ┦ ⥪ 樧
   liability to be discharged before the settlement date if it is    瑟⺭萵䇕卋♭侇⩒錊㴨냇⺎㵩✳榫꧇
   settled in cash using an electronic payment system if, and        㯹♀堑硎礵♓杯ꄌ磶畁╯ꄌ踅靷⥍釲憡
   only if certain conditions are met.                               亦磶畁仼僿╯⯥⨑♀

   The amendments also provide guidance on the assessment            鎊璣⟣銬儗◱㶩鍑⚽ꄌ踅鞳榣╯⺭硝杯
   of whether the contractual cash flows of a financial asset        ꄌ孲ꄈ佭⻖艃㓹儗⠺鞚㱨䪶注┉舜䬠❠
   are consistent with a basic lending arrangement. The              䧘䌖鎊璣⟣銬儗銬伺㴨냇䟕樧㵬嫱亦
   amendments specify that an entity should focus on what an         暢䐃適⨑氳㴨냇縡ꪞ適⨑ꄌ갯萵⺭硝
   entity is being compensated for rather than the compensation      杯ꄌ孲ꄈ艃┾ꪞ㓹儗⠺鞚겨ꤠ䡝䡗儗氳
   amount. Contractual cash flows are inconsistent with a            闭ꄈ䫇ꅺ⯞⪭艃㓹儗⠺鞚㱨䪶┘┉舜
   basic lending arrangement if they are indexed to a variable       鎊璣⟣銬儗ꢷ鳯亦萵䇕䗯媲┖䡝抈攑
   that is not a basic lending risk or cost. The amendments          䏣⺎耉亦⺭硝杯ꄌ孲ꄈ闭Ⳃ╯⯥⹖╯䏳
   state that, in some cases, a contingent feature may give          䌖舜艃㓹儗鞚堑㱨䪶┉舜╯⺭硝杯ꄌ孲
   rise to contractual cash flows that are consistent with a         ꄈ䘞䡝抈◄♭儗骰╯䓪韭艃㓹儗⠺鞚
   basic lending arrangement both before and after the change        겨ꤠ⹖䡗儗╯闭⴫┾┘泣䫚注꟯塝㛛
   in contractual cash flows, but the nature of the contingent       鎊璣⟣銬儗╈ⱶ䍛㵴ր扝鴓碠垷ց┉鍢╯
   event itself does not relate directly to changes in basic         䬟鳯♓⹖ꄊ岳ր⺭硝注꟯䄄ց╯攑럅
   lending risks and costs. Furthermore, the description of the
   term “non-recourse” is enhanced and the characteristics
   of “contractually linked instruments” are clarified in the
   amendments.

   The disclosure requirements in HKFRS 7 “Financial                 꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮7谝րꄌ踅䄄䥑
   Instruments: Disclosures” in respect of investments in            ꨾ց僗꟯䧘㲋憡鵂鷪⪭☾ꪫ䷅沠䧞⪝
   equity instruments designated at fair value through other         䇖⡭⮛韰╯垷沠䄄╯䤸鞳╯䥑꨾釨
   comprehensive income are amended. In particular, entities         㲋 䄖 ◀ ⟣ 銬攑 ⮯ 佭㴨 냇 ꯄ 䥑 ꨾ 亦 僿
   are required to disclose the fair value gain or loss presented    ⪐⪭☾ꪫ䷅沠⪐⮛╯⪝䇖⡭䷅沠
   in other comprehensive income during the period, showing          䡝谱䮇⮇⮯⮛炐艃亦㕕⼔僿⪐䄖䱨ꢝ
   separately those related to investments derecognised during       澭鏂╯䤸鞳注꟯縖♓⹖艃亦㕕⼔僿儕䧏
   the reporting period and those related to investments held at     僗╯䤸鞳注꟯縖㴨냇◱ꯄ䥑꨾亦㕕⼔
   the end of the reporting period. An entity is also required to    僿⪐䄖䱨ꢝ澭鏂䤸鞳注꟯╯垷沠⪐碶銷
   disclose any transfers of the cumulative gain or loss within      ䷅沠䡝谱䮇╯♴⛰鮺䲡塝㛛鎊璣⟣銬
   equity related to the investments derecognised during the         儗䌖鶡㵴⺎耉䏆ꮷ㓹亦䡝抈㍕碜ⷊ✳
   reporting period. In addition, the amendments introduce the       艃㓹儗⠺鞚겨ꤠ⹖䡗儗┘泣䫚注꟯╯
   requirements of qualitative and quantitative disclosure of        ⺭硝杯ꄌ孲ꄈ╯⺭硝卋堑鶡车㲋䓪⹖㲋
   contractual terms that could affect the contractual cash flow     ꄈ䥑꨾╯釒姲
   based on a contingent event not directly relating to basic
   lending risks and cost.

   Statements”                                                       ⮛⹖䥑꨾ց
   HKFRS 18 Presentation and Disclosure in Financial                 꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮18谝靸ⳉ㕕辑╯
   Statements, which sets out requirements on presentation           ⮛⹖䥑꨾鬼⮛靸ⳉ㕕辑╯⮛⹖䥑꨾釨
   and disclosures in financial statements, will replace HKAS        㲋㵩⹦♏꺞僎銷嶗⯞瑮1谝ր靸ⳉ㕕
   1 “Presentation of Financial Statements”. This new HKFRS          辑 ╯  ⮛ց儗 亏 銬 꺞  靸 ⳉ 㕕 ⼔ 嶗 ⯞
   Accounting Standard, while carrying forward many of the           僎銷嶗⯞㏇䋟箜꺞僎銷嶗⯞瑮1谝╈
   requirements in HKAS 1, introduces new requirements               浑㛡釨㲋╯⺱侇䌖⪌亦䮇沠辑╈⮛
   to present specified categories and defined subtotals in          䧘㲋⮯⹖㲋紶㵸銷╯亏釨㲋㶩靸ⳉ
   the statement of profit or loss; provide disclosures on           㕕辑ꡭ錝╈畚杼㷼槡㲋╯辑杯銷ꄈ䬠❠
   management-defined performance measures in the notes              䥑꨾⹖䷊鶡亦靸ⳉ㕕辑╈㵩◀䥑꨾╯⺭
   to the financial statements and improve aggregation and           ✡⹖⮇鞳乢塝㛛꺞僎銷嶗⯞瑮1
   disaggregation of information to be disclosed in the financial    谝╯鼨⮇夀蛾䄖燯舘꺞僎銷嶗⯞瑮8
   statements. In addition, some HKAS 1 paragraphs have              谝ր僎銷䷓瓁僎銷⚽銷╯闭Ⳃ⹖ꎭ鏲ց
   been moved to HKAS 8 “Accounting Policies, Changes in             ⹖ 꺞  靸 ⳉ 㕕 ⼔ 嶗 ⯞ 瑮 7 谝꺞  僎 銷
   Accounting Estimates and Errors” and HKFRS 7. Minor               嶗⯞瑮7谝ր杯ꄌ孲ꄈ辑ց⹖꺞僎銷嶗
   amendments to HKAS 7 “Statement of Cash Flows” and HKAS           ⯞瑮33谝ր夷罇沜⮵ց◱⛼⭴碸䐮⟣銬
   33 “Earnings per Share” are also made.

   HKFRS 18, and amendments to other standards, will be              꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮18谝⹖⪭☾嶗⯞╯
   effective for annual periods beginning on or after 1 January      ⟣銬儗㵩亦2027䇗1僖1仼䡝╯䏳ꝧ㡐
   2027, with early application permitted. The application of        ╯䇗䈲僿ꝴ榟䷞┾⩒錊䬠企䟕榫䟕榫
   the new standard is expected to affect the presentation           亏嶗⯞ꯏ僿㵩僎䏆ꮷ䮇沠辑╯⮛♓⹖
   of the statement of profit or loss and disclosures in the         儔✿靸ⳉ㕕辑╯䥑꨾儗꥘㎯塜㏇鍑⚽
   future financial statements. The Group is in the process of       꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮18谝㵴儗꥘㎯禍⺭
   assessing the detailed impact of HKFRS 18 on the Group’s          靸ⳉ㕕辑╯鎌碸䏆ꮷ
   consolidated financial statements.

   Statements and Material Accounting Policy                             㛻僎銷䷓瓁鞳乢
   Information
   Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                      禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑╯稵邯㓹嶗
   statements
   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in         禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑╤劯䴆꺞僎銷䅨⪝僎ꯓ
   accordance with HKFRS Accounting Standards issued by the            ⛜╯꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞僎銷嶗⯞稵邯
   HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of the consolidated          㶩稵邯禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑縡銨⠭⟔䕜⺭杼
   financial statements, information is considered material            ꯏ僿僎䏆ꮷ╚釒榫䢒⛼⭴氳婋㲋⯞僗
   if such information is reasonably expected to influence             ꟯⟔䕜逈釲憡ꄆ㛻塝㛛禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑
   decisions made by primary users. In addition, the consolidated      鬼僗꺞绤⺭◬佄䢦僗꡿⪝⺚镜⯉┕䄬
   financial statements include applicable disclosures required        釨⯞ր┕䄬釨⯞ց⹖꺞⪝⺚卋❆䢦釨
   by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock       㲋╯鸸榫䥑꨾鞳乢
   Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”) and by
   the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared            禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑╤䧞塽⺒䡗儗㓹嶗稵邯
   on the historical cost basis except for certain financial           䘞㞞┖乄䢦鬼╯僎銷䷓瓁䢦鳯萵䇕ꄌ踅
   instruments that are measured at fair value at the end of           䄄亦⺨㕕⼔僿儕䧞⪝䇖⡭銷ꄈꢝ㛛
   each reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies
   set out below.

   Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the         塽⺒䡗儗┉芙㓹亦㶩◬䬷鞁⿁䡝僜ⳉ䢦
   consideration given in exchange for goods and services.             礢⭴╯♏⧎╯⪝䇖⡭

   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an           ⪝䇖⡭憡䄬㕙⹉艃縖亦銷ꄈ仼僿㏇僗䈌
   asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction     ◬佄╈⭴す鞳榣㵩䷅⹦䡝鮺阁靷⥍㵩䶺
   between market participants at the measurement date,                ♀╯⧎劲┘鑝鎊⧎劲佭⻖泣䫚鈶㴕⺎
   regardless of whether that price is directly observable or          䐃䡝✳榫⺁┉牊⚽⡭亡嫏⚽銷⚽銷鞳
   estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating          榣䡝靷⥍╯⪝䇖⡭侇儗꥘㎯僎縓䜢䄬
   the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into     㕙⹉艃縖亦銷ꄈ仼僿㵴鞳榣䡝靷⥍㲋⧎
   account the characteristics of the asset or liability if market     侇㵩僎縓䜢氳鞳榣䡝靷⥍攑䐹㏇鎊璣
   participants would take those characteristics into account          禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑╈銷ꄈ⹖ҧ䡝䥑꨾╯⪝䇖
   when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement date.        ⡭㏱㏇塝㓹灅┕◀♓澭㲋䘞㸋꺞靸
   Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in            ⳉ 㕕 ⼔ 嶗 ⯞ 瑮 2 谝ր♓ 罇 ♷ 憡 㓹 灅 ╯ ♀
   these consolidated financial statements is determined on            堑ց疘 ㎠ ⪐ ╯ ♓ 罇 ♷ 憡 㓹 灅 ♀ 堑 ◬ 佄
   such a basis, except for share-based payment transactions           劯 䴆 꺞  靸 ⳉ 㕕 ⼔ 嶗 ⯞ 瑮 1 6 谝ր燃 鞫ց
   that are within the scope of HKFRS 2 “Share-based Payment”,         ⪌韰╯燃鞫◬佄⹖艃⪝䇖⡭⛍⛙┾
   leasing transactions that are accounted for in accordance           ꪞ⪝䇖⡭╯銷ꄈ❆㞞꺞僎銷嶗⯞瑮
   with HKFRS 16 “Leases”, measurements that have some                 36谝ր鞳榣峎⡭ց╈╯✳榫⧎⡭ꢝ㛛
   similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as value
   in use in HKAS 36 “Impairment of Assets”.

   Basis of consolidation                                                禍⺭㓹嶗
   The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                 禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑ⴎ䦮儗⪝⺚⹖儗⪝⺚䢦䫞
   financial statements of the Company and entities controlled           ⯆㴨냇⹖⪭ꡭ㸋⪝⺚╯靸ⳉ㕕辑䳕僗
   by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved              䫞⯆垷䧘儗⪝⺚
   when the Company:

   •   has power over the investee;                                      •   僗垷䫞⯆逈䤸鞳亡

   •   is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its           •   䤖䳸䡝◸僗⹉艃逈䤸鞳亡䢦䐃⺎闭
       involvement with the investee; and                                    ㍑㕕氳겨ꤠ䡝垷⮵⹖

   •   has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.           •   僗耉ⱱ车✳⪭垷ⱱ䏆ꮷ⪭㍑㕕ꄌ
                                                                             갯

   The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee           ⠭◄㴨⹖䗯媲辑伺┕⮛└ꯁ䫞⯆㍕碜
   if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes            ╈僗┉ꯁ䡝㛡ꯁ⭴杯闭Ⳃ⯞儗꥘㎯僎
   to one or more of the three elements of control listed above.         ꄆ⚽佭⻖❤抈㵴逈䤸鞳亡䧏僗䫞⯆垷

   Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains           ꡭ㸋⪝⺚氳禍⺭⪌韰亦儗꥘㎯暢䐃㵴ꡭ
   control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses           㸋⪝⺚╯䫞⯆垷侇ꝧ㡐┾亦儗꥘㎯㜊
   control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses          ⸼㵴ꡭ㸋⪝⺚╯䫞⯆垷侇磚塛냇縡
   of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are           銨亦䇗⪐䷅頇䡝⭴す╯ꡭ㸋⪝⺚╯䷅
   included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and          ⪌⹖ꝧ䶺銷⪌舅儗꥘㎯暢䐃䫞⯆垷╯仼
   other comprehensive income from the date the Group gains              泣舘儗꥘㎯┘⫋䫞⯆ꡭ㸋⪝⺚╯仼╯禍
   control until the date when the Group ceases to control the           ⺭䮇沠⹖⪭☾ꪫ䷅沠辑
   subsidiary.

   When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial                 ⠭僗ꧏ釒㵩㵴ꡭ㸋⪝⺚╯靸ⳉ㕕辑⛼
   statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies         ⭴鐪丞♓✳⪭僎銷䷓瓁艃儗꥘㎯╯僎
   in line with the Group’s accounting policies.                         銷䷓瓁┉舜

   All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses      艃儗꥘㎯䡗⿦ꝴ╯◬佄僗꟯╯䢦僗꥘㎯
   and cash flows relating to transactions between members of            ⪐鞳榣⹖靷⥍垷沠䷅⪌ꝧ䶺⹖杯ꄌ
   the Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                    孲ꄈ亦禍⺭⪌韰侇䖅丩㵴ꊷ

   Business combinations                                                 哅ⳉ⺭✡
   A business is an integrated set of activities and assets which        哅ⳉ佭┉磞㱮丞哅ⳉ⹖鞳榣⪭ⴎ䦮┉
   includes an input and a substantive process that together             ꯁ䤸⪌⹖┉ꯁ㴨韭孲⪦⺱걦蜸➢鶡
   significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs. The        Ⱚ鵴榣⭴╯耉ⱱ⠭䢦䷅頇╯孲㵴䧏
   acquired processes are considered substantive if they are             箜榣⭴╯耉ⱱ舘꟯ꄆ釒ⴎ䦮⤔㓵车
   critical to the ability to continue producing outputs, including      注꟯孲䢦ꧏ䤗耉湳閂䡝祺꿨氳僗磞
   an organised workforce with the necessary skills, knowledge,          笞 Ⳓ Ⳃ ⱱ
                                                                                䡝 걦 蜸 ➢ 鶡 Ⱚ 鵴 䧏 箜 榣 ⭴
   or experience to perform the related processes or they                ╯耉ⱱ┾逈鏂憡㸋暐攑䡝燵籭䡝┘♀
   significantly contribute to the ability to continue producing         ⭴ꄆ㛻䡗儗ⲅⱱ䡝䋟鹏䧏箜榣⭴╯耉
   outputs and are considered unique or scarce or cannot be              ⱱ⯞扝嫏僂♏⯞鎊璣孲逈釲憡㴨韭
   replaced without significant cost, effort, or delay in the ability    孲
   to continue producing outputs.

   Business combinations (Continued)                                   哅ⳉ⺭✡箜
   Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the              哅ⳉ䷅頇╤䫑榫䷅頇嫏⪌韰哅ⳉ⺭✡
   acquisition method. The consideration transferred in a              鮺阁╯♏⧎䧞⪝䇖⡭銷ꄈ縡銷畁亡嫏
   business combination is measured at fair value, which is            憡儗꥘㎯䢦鮺阁╯鞳榣儗꥘㎯⺸逈䷅
   calculated as the sum of the acquisition-date fair values of        頇亡氳⯥䳕僗☕榣榟╯靷⥍♓⹖儗꥘
   the assets transferred by the Group, liabilities incurred by the    ㎯憡䬷⹦逈䷅頇亡╯䫞⯆垷縡氪车╯垷
   Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity           沠亦䷅頇仼僿╯⪝䇖⡭竬⽰䷅頇注꟯
   interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of the        䡗儗┉芙亦榣榟侇㏇䮇沠╈澭鏂
   acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally recognised
   in profit or loss as incurred.

   The identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed            䢦䷅頇╯⺎閂⮯鞳榣⹖䢦䤖䳸╯靷⥍䑒
   must meet the definitions of an asset and a liability in            ꯄ瑟⺭ 靸ⳉ㕕⼔哯䒟勂几ր哯䒟勂几ց
   the Conceptual Framework for Financial Reporting (the               ╈鞳榣⹖靷⥍╯㲋紶䘞㸋亦꺞僎銷
   “Conceptual Framework”) except for transactions and events          嶗⯞瑮37谝 䲡⤔䡝僗靷⥍⹖䡝僗鞳榣
   within the scope of HKAS 37 Provisions, Contingent Liabilities      䡝꺞㎜꤀靸ⳉ㕕⼔鎃ꄄ㡨⿦僎鎃ꄄ
   and Contingent Assets or HK(IFRIC)-Int 21 Levies , in which         瑮21谝 䐹鞞 疘㎠⪐╯◬佄⹖◄ꯁꢝ㛛
   the Group applies HKAS 37 or HK(IFRIC)-Int 21 instead of            亦鎊璣䗯媲┖儗꥘㎯䟕榫꺞僎銷嶗
   the Conceptual Framework to identify the liabilities it has         ⯞瑮37谝䡝꺞㎜꤀靸ⳉ㕕⼔鎃ꄄ㡨
   assumed in a business combination. Contingent assets are            ⿦僎鎃ꄄ瑮21谝縡ꪞ哯䒟勂几♓閂
   not recognised.                                                     ⮯⪭亦哅ⳉ⺭✡╈䄖䤖䳸╯靷⥍䡝僗
                                                                       鞳榣┘◀澭鏂

   At the acquisition date, the identifiable assets acquired and       亦䷅頇仼僿䢦䷅頇╯⺎閂⮯鞳榣⹖䢦
   the liabilities assumed are recognised at their fair value.         䤖䳸╯靷⥍䧞⪭⪝䇖⡭澭鏂

   Goodwill is measured as the excess of the sum of the                り闌䧞䢦鮺阁♏⧎亦逈䷅頇亡╯♴⛰
   consideration transferred, the amount of any non-controlling        ꪞ䫞罇垷沠╯ꄌ갯⹖䷅頇亡⩝⯥䧏僗
   interests in the acquiree, and the fair value of the acquirer’s     逈䷅頇亡垷沠╯⪝䇖⡭㞞僗╯竬⽰
   previously held equity interest in the acquiree (if any) over       飃⭴䢦䷅頇╯⺎閂⮯鞳榣⹖䢦䤖䳸╯
   the net amount of the identifiable assets acquired and              靷⥍亦䷅頇仼僿╯ꄌ갯屻⡭銷畁⠭祺
   the liabilities assumed as at acquisition date. If, after           ꄆ⚽䏳䢦䷅頇⺎閂⮯鞳榣⹖䢦䤖䳸╯
   reassessment, the net amount of the identifiable assets             靷⥍╯ꄌ갯屻⡭飃⭴䢦鮺阁♏⧎亦逈
   acquired and liabilities assumed exceeds the sum of the             ䷅頇亡╯♴⛰ꪞ䫞罇垷沠ꄌ갯⹖䷅頇亡
   consideration transferred, the amount of any non-controlling        ⩝⯥䧏僗逈䷅頇亡垷沠╯⪝䇖⡭㞞僗
   interests in the acquiree and the fair value of the acquirer’s      ╯竬⽰⯞䄐갯ⷊ侇亦䮇沠澭鏂憡閲⧎
   previously held interest in the acquiree (if any), the excess       ䷅頇䷅沠
   is recognised immediately in profit or loss as a bargain
   purchase gain.

   Information (Continued)
   Material accounting policy information (Continued)                            ꄆ㛻僎銷䷓瓁鞳乢箜
   Fair value measurement                                                        ⪝䇖⡭銷ꄈ
   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an                     ⪝䇖⡭憡䄬㕙⹉艃縖亦銷ꄈ仼僿㏇僗䈌
   asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction               ◬佄╈⭴す鞳榣㵩䷅⹦䡝鮺阁靷⥍㵩䶺
   between market participants at the measurement date.                          ♀╯⧎劲⪝䇖⡭銷ꄈ╤㓹亦⡾錄⭴す
   The fair value measurement is based on the presumption                        鞳榣䡝鮺阁靷⥍╯◬佄亦鞳榣䡝靷⥍╯
   that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability              ╚釒䄬㕙䡝㏇儔僗╚釒䄬㕙╯䗯媲┖
   takes place either in the principal market for the asset or                   ⯞亦鞳榣䡝靷⥍╯僅僗⮵䄬㕙鶡车╚
   liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most               釒䡝僅僗⮵䄬㕙ꯄ⛤亦儗꥘㎯耉⮽鷾╯
   advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal                 ㏑亡鞳榣䡝靷⥍╯⪝䇖⡭╤✳榫䄬㕙
   or the most advantageous market must be accessible by the                     ⹉艃縖憡鞳榣䡝靷⥍㲋⧎䢦榫╯⡾錄銷
   Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured                  ꄈ⡾錄䄬㕙⹉艃縖❤拨䏘璣╯僅✞祺
   using the assumptions that market participants would use                      忴⮵沠车◄
   when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market
   participants act in their economic best interest.

   A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                  ꪞꄌ踅鞳榣╯⪝䇖⡭銷ꄈ╤祺銷⹖┉⺲
   account a market participant’s ability to generate economic                   䄬㕙⹉艃縖鵂鷪✳榫⪭鞳榣╯僅냖⹖僅
   benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by                 ✞榫鵐䡝鵂鷪㵩鞳榣⭴す◀㵩✳榫⪭僅
   selling it to another market participant that would use the                   냖⹖僅✞榫鵐╯⺁┉⺲䄬㕙⹉艃縖縡耉
   asset in its highest and best use.                                            㛭榣榟祺忴⮵沠╯耉ⱱ

   The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                   儗꥘㎯✳榫鸸榫亦┘⺱䗯媲╯⚽⡭䤗
   the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available                 轱縡 ⪭ 僗 餉 㛭 丩 䴆 銷 ꄈ ⪝ 䇖 ⡭♓ 況
   to measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant                         ꄈ⮵榫注꟯⺎鈶㴕鮗⪌丩䴆⹖況ꄈ峎
   observable inputs and minimising the use of unobservable                      㵼✳榫┘⺎鈶㴕鮗⪌丩䴆
   inputs.

   All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or                亦靸ⳉ㕕辑銷ꄈ䡝䥑꨾⪝䇖⡭╯䢦僗鞳
   disclosed in the financial statements are categorised within                  榣⹖靷⥍╤⹉拨⚽⡭䤗轱䢦榫鮗⪌丩
   the fair value hierarchy with reference to the observability                  䴆╯⺎鈶㴕䓪⹖ꄆ釒䓪㏇┖鳯⪝䇖⡭璣
   and significance of the inputs that are used in the valuation                 碍几啶⪐鶡车⮇
   technique as follows:

   – Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only                          – 瑮1碍⚽⡭ ⥪ ✳ 榫 瑮 ┉ 碍 鮗 ⪌ 丩
                         Level 1 inputs i.e. unadjusted                                   䴆銷ꄈ╯⪝䇖⡭ⷊ亦
                         quoted prices in active markets for                              銷ꄈ仼僿注⺱鞳榣䡝靷
                         identical assets or liabilities at the                           ⥍㏇孨驳䄬㕙╯儔祺鐪
                         measurement date                                                 丞㕕⧎

   – Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level                         – 瑮2碍⚽⡭ ✳榫瑮◅碍鮗⪌丩䴆銷
                         2 i n p u t s i . e . o b s e r va b l e i n p u t s             ꄈ╯⪝䇖⡭ⷊ┘瑟⺭
                         which fail to meet Level 1, and                                  瑮┉碍╯⺎鈶㴕鮗⪌丩
                         not using significant unobservable                               䴆┦儔僗✳榫ꄆ㛻┘⺎
                         inputs. Unobser vable inputs are                                 鈶㴕鮗⪌丩䴆┘⺎鈶
                         inputs for which market data are                                 㴕鮗⪌丩䴆䧘扝嫏⹦
                         not available                                                    䐃䄬㕙鞳乢╯鮗⪌丩䴆

   – Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant                   – 瑮3碍⚽⡭ ✳榫ꄆ㛻┘⺎鈶㴕鮗⪌
                         unobservable inputs                                              丩䴆銷ꄈ╯⪝䇖⡭

   Revenue from contracts with customers                                ✿舅㲙䢒⺭硝╯䷅⪌
   The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance              儗꥘㎯亦㱮䡗㷿硝鞊♴侇䡝㶩塝澭鏂
   obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or         ䷅⪌ⷊ亦攑㲋㷿硝鞊♴注꟯鞁⿁䡝僜
   services underlying the particular performance obligation is         ⳉ╯ր䫞⯆垷ց鮺燯舘㲙䢒侇
   transferred to the customer.

   A performance obligation represents a good or service (or            㷿硝鞊♴䧘┉ꯁ伺澭鞁⿁䡝僜ⳉ䡝┉
   a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of       䤊鞁⿁䡝僜ⳉ䡝┉硎⮛㛻舜注⺱╯伺
   distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.          澭鞁⿁䡝僜ⳉ

   Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised           䫞⯆垷ꤛ侇ꝴ鮺燯㞞幥餉♓┖⪭╈┉
   over time by reference to the progress towards complete              ꯁ嘅嶗⯞䷅⪌僎䧞拨㱮幥餉注꟯㷿
   satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of        硝鞊♴氳鶡㷤䗯媲縡ꤛ侇ꝴ澭鏂
   the following criteria is met:

   •   the customer simultaneously receives and consumes                •   㲙䢒亦儗꥘㎯㷿硝侇⺱侇䷅⹦⹖寕
       the benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the              縶儗꥘㎯㷿硝䢦䬠❠╯⮵沠
       Group performs;

   •   the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset             •   儗꥘㎯㷿硝榣榟▽䡝䍛⴫▽鞳榣
       that the customer controls as the Group performs; or                 縡鎊鞳榣氳䫞⯆垷㏇儗꥘㎯㷿硝侇
                                                                            佭㸋亦㲙䢒氳䡝

   •   the Group’s performance does not create an asset with            •   儗꥘㎯㷿硝儔Ⱚ䋧㵴儗꥘㎯僗僂
       an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                 ♏榫鵐╯鞳榣縡儗꥘㎯䳕僗⺎䍛
       enforceable right to payment for performance completed               ⯆㓵车垷⮵䷅⹦鲄☬䄖㷿硝鼨⮇╯
       to date.                                                             堑ꯁ

   Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the         ⻖⯞䷅⪌僎㏇㲙䢒暢䐃伺澭鞁⿁䡝僜
   customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.            ⳉ氳䫞⯆垷侇澭鏂

   A contract asset represents the Group’s right to consideration       ⺭硝鞳榣䧘儗꥘㎯♓鞁⿁䡝僜ⳉ䬷⹦♏
   in exchange for goods or services that the Group has                 ⧎氳垷⮵縡儗꥘㎯䄖鮺阁礢㲙䢒氳鞁
   transferred to a customer that is not yet unconditional. It          ⿁䡝僜ⳉ☱┘佭扝卋♭氳⺭硝鞳榣佭
   is assessed for impairment in accordance with HKFRS 9. In            劯䴆꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮9谝鍑⚽峎⡭
   contrast, a receivable represents the Group’s unconditional          注⹛䟕䷅堑ꯁ䧘儗꥘㎯䷅⹦♏⧎氳扝
   right to consideration, i.e. only the passage of time is required    卋♭垷⮵ⷊ♏⧎⥪ꤛ侇ꝴ䫠燯ⷊ僎䡗
   before payment of that consideration is due.                         憡⮽僿䶺♀

   with the variable consideration is subsequently resolved.

   in circumstances during the reporting period.

       and conditions of the lease.

   Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any                 ✳榫垷鞳榣䧞䡗儗銷ꄈ峎⸼♴⛰碶銷
   accumulated depreciation and impairment losses, and                䤽艋⹖峎⡭谱䮇┾㶩燃鞫靷⥍氳♴⛰
   adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities. Right-        ꄆ亏銷ꄈ⛼⭴鐪丞✳榫垷鞳榣㏇⚽銷
   of-use assets are depreciated on a straight-line basis over        ⺎✳榫䇗僿艃燃僿⪔縖㰵湿氳僿ꝴ⪐䧞
   the shorter of its estimated useful life and the lease term.       泣稘嫏銷䬠䤽艋

   The Group presents right-of-use assets as a separate line          儗꥘㎯亦禍⺭靸ⳉ敻媲辑⪐㵩✳榫垷鞳
   item on the consolidated statement of financial position.          榣⮛憡ㄲ暐ꯁ泘

   Refundable rental deposits                                         ⺎鴛㍑╯燃鞫䧞ꄌ
   Refundable rental deposits paid are accounted under HKFRS          䄖䶺♀氳⺎鴛㍑燃鞫䧞ꄌ╤劯䴆꺞
   9 and initially measured at fair value. Adjustments to fair        靸 ⳉ 㕕 ⼔ 嶗 ⯞ 瑮 9 谝 ⪌ 韰┾ ⮤ 塠 䧞 ⪝
   value at initial recognition are considered as additional lease    䇖⡭銷ꄈ⮤㡐澭鏂侇㵴⪝䇖⡭氳鐪丞
   payments and included in the cost of right-of-use assets.          逈釲憡갯㛛燃鞫♀堑┾銷⪌✳榫垷鞳
                                                                      榣䡗儗

   Lease liabilities                                                  燃鞫靷⥍
   At the commencement date of a lease, the Group recognises          亦燃鞫ꝧ㡐仼僿儗꥘㎯䧞鎊仼儔䶺♀
   and measures the lease liability at the present value of           氳燃鞫♀堑杯⡭澭鏂⹖銷ꄈ燃鞫靷⥍
   lease payments that are unpaid at that date. In calculating        亦銷畁燃鞫♀堑杯⡭侇⠭燃鞫꤬⻢氳
   the present value of lease payments, the Group uses the            ⮵曎ꦙ♓ꄊ㲋⯞儗꥘㎯✳榫燃鞫ꝧ㡐
   incremental borrowing rate at the lease commencement               仼僿氳㘎ꄈ⠺堑⮵曎銷畁
   date if the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily
   determinable.

   receivable.

   by interest accretion and lease payments.                       ⹖燃鞫♀堑⛼⭴鐪丞

   whenever:

   •   the lease term has changed, in which case the related       •   鎊燃僿䄖䷊闭㏇塝䗯媲┖注꟯燃
       lease liability is remeasured by discounting the revised        鞫靷⥍亦ꄆ亏鍑⚽仼僿♓祺⟣銬鞟

       of reassessment.                                                ꄈ

   •   the lease payments change due to changes in market          •   燃鞫♀堑㍕鶡车䄬㕙燃ꄌ㴱ꞥ䏳䄬
       rental rates following a market rent review, in which           㕙燃ꄌ僗闭縡⭴杯闭Ⳃ亦塝䗯媲
       cases the related lease liability is remeasured by              ┖注꟯燃鞫靷⥍♓⮤㡐鞟杯曎鞟
       discounting the revised lease payments using the initial        杯祺⟣銬燃鞫♀堑縡ꄆ亏銷ꄈ
       discount rate.

   lease if:                                                       憡┉ꯁㄲ暐氳燃鞫鶡车⪌韰

       adding the right to use one or more underlying assets;          注꟯鞳榣氳垷⮵䴺㛻燃鞫疘㎠⹖
       and

       commensurate with the stand-alone price for the                 亦疘㎠䴺㛻㵴䟕氳暐环⧎劲ⱶ┕
       increase in scope and any appropriate adjustments to            䧞拨攑㲋⺭硝氳㴨꤀䗯媲㵴暐环⧎

   of property and equipment less their residual values over          榫䇗僿䱨ꊷ⪭ꯁ泘䡗儗⡭峎⪭Ⱂ궠⧎⡭
   their estimated useful lives, using the straight-line method.      澭鏂⚽銷⺎✳榫䇗僿Ⱂ궠⧎⡭⹖䤽艋
   The estimated useful lives, residual values and depreciation       亡嫏亦⺨㕕⼔僿儕鶡车㴱ꞥ縡♴⛰⚽
   method are reviewed at the end of each reporting period,           銷闭Ⳃ╯䏆ꮷ䧞⯥渥㓹嶗⪌韰

   to arise from the continued use of the asset. Any gain or loss     沠侇鎊ꯁ鞳榣◀♓䱨ꢝ澭鏂⭴す䡝厐
   arising on the disposal or retirement of an item of property       榫┉ꯁ擻哅⹖錄⤔ꯁ泘榣榟╯♴⛰䷅沠
   and equipment is determined as the difference between the          䡝谱䮇ꄊ㲋憡鞳榣ꊷす䢦䐃堑ꯁ艃韰ꪫ
   sales proceeds and the carrying amount of the asset and is         ⡭╯䄐갯┾亦䮇沠⪐澭鏂

   Foreign currencies                                                 㛛䆸
   In preparing the financial statements of each individual group     ㏇稵邯⺨⠕⮯꥘㎯㴨냇╯靸ⳉ㕕辑侇
   entity, transactions in currencies other than the functional       ♓鎊㴨냇╯Ⱶ耉鞁䆸♓㛛╯鞁䆸㛛䆸
   currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are recognised        銷⧎╯◬佄╤䧞◬佄仼僿╯杯车ⵜ曎澭
   at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates of the           鏂㏇㕕⼔僿儕♓㛛䆸憡ㄲ⛤╯鞁䆸䓪
   transactions. At the end of the reporting period, monetary         ꯁ泘㏱䧞鎊仼╯杯车ⵜ曎ꄆ亏䬷畁䧞
   items denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at        㛛䆸╯塽⺒䡗儗銷ꄈ╯ꪞ鞁䆸ꯁ泘┘◀
   the rates prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items that are     ♓ꄆ亏䬷畁
   measured in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are
   not retranslated.

   Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary         榺磶畁鞁䆸䓪ꯁ泘⹖ꄆ亏䬷畁鞁䆸䓪ꯁ
   items, and on the retranslation of monetary items, are             泘縡榣榟╯ⵜ⩣䄐갯亦䏘璣榣榟僿ꝴ
   recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they arise.    ㏇䮇沠╈澭鏂

   For the purposes of presenting the consolidated financial          ⮛㕕禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑侇儗꥘㎯宿㛛哅ⳉ
   statements, the assets and liabilities of the Group’s foreign      ╯鞳榣⹖靷⥍䧞⺨㕕⼔僿儕╯杯车ⵜ
   operations are translated into the presentation currency of        曎䬷畁憡儗꥘㎯╯⮛㕕鞁䆸ⷊ⩕
   the Group (i.e. Hong Kong dollars) using exchange rates            ䷅⪌⹖䶺⭴ꯁ泘⯞䧞䇗⪐╯䇖㏱ⵜ曎
   prevailing at the end of each reporting period. Income and         䬷畁䢦榣榟╯ⵜ⩣䄐갯㞞僗亦⪭☾
   expenses items are translated at the average exchange              ꪫ䷅沠⪐澭鏂┾亦䬷畁⨶⤔ꯁ┖╯
   rates for the year. Exchange differences arising, if any, are      垷沠碶銷
   recognised in other comprehensive income and accumulated
   in equity under the heading of translation reserve.

   On the disposal of a foreign operation (that is, a disposal        ⭴す宿㛛哅ⳉ侇ⷊ⭴す儗꥘㎯亦宿㛛
   of the Group’s entire interest in a foreign operation, or a        哅ⳉ╯鼨垷沠䡝⭴す寘⹖㜊⸼㵴ⴎ
   disposal involving loss of control over a subsidiary that          ⻢ 宿 㛛 哅 ⳉ 氳 ꡭ 㸋 ⪝ ⺚ ╯ 䫞 ⯆ 垷
                                                                                                 亦
   includes a foreign operation), all of the exchange differences     垷沠╈碶銷┾艃鎊哅ⳉ注꟯╯塾㸋亦
   accumulated in equity in respect of that operation attributable    儗⪝⺚䳕僗☕╯䢦僗ⵜ⩣䄐갯ꄆ亏⮇
   to the owners of the Company are reclassified to profit or         舘䮇沠
   loss.

   with a corresponding adjustment to the share-based                  ⷊ侇塾㸋╯頇罇垷縡銨䢦䪧⭴頇罇垷

   Bonus plans                                                         菍硟銷ⱁ
   The Group recognises a liability and an expense for bonuses,        儗꥘㎯䧞拨鏂⺎銷畁⪝䌋㶩菍硟澭鏂靷
   where appropriate, based on approved formulas that take             ⥍⹖ꝧ䶺㞞鸸榫
                                                                               鎊⪝䌋䄖銷⹖儗꥘
   into consideration the profit attributable to the Group after       ㎯䟕⛯嶯⮵⛼⭴萵䇕鐪丞䏳
                                                                                    儗꥘㎯
   certain adjustments. The Group recognises a provision where         㶩⺭硝鞊♴䡝䴆鷪䏢祺꿨䄖榣榟䫠㲋鞊
   contractually obliged or where there is a past practice that        ♴縡澭鏂䲡⤔
   has created a constructive obligation.

   Employment benefit (Continued)                                     ⧀⿦焱⮵箜
   All other short-term employee benefits                             䢦僗⪭☾湿僿⧀⿦焱⮵
   Short-term employee benefits are recognised at the                 湿僿⧀⿦焱⮵亦⧀⿦䬠❠僜ⳉ侇䧞ꯏ僿
   undiscounted amount of the benefits expected to be paid as         暢䶺♀氳焱⮵儔鞟杯堑ꯁ澭鏂䢦僗湿
   and when employees rendered the services. All short-term           僿⧀⿦焱⮵㏱澭鏂憡ꝧ䶺䘞⺁┉ꯁ꺞
   employee benefits are recognised as an expense unless              靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞釨㲋䡝⩒錊㵩鎊ꯁ焱⮵
   another HKFRS requires or permits the inclusion of the             亦鞳榣䡗儗⪌韰縖⯞ꢝ㛛靷⥍╤䧞䣩
   benefit in the cost of an asset. A liability is recognised for     峎♴⛰䄖♀堑ꯁ䏳⧀⿦䟕⛯焱⮵❆㞞
   benefits accruing to employees (such as wages and salaries,        䄄鞳⹖詯ꄌ♓⹖䇗⡾澭鏂
   and annual leave) after deducting any amount already paid.

   Borrowing costs                                                    ⠺堑䡗儗
   All borrowing costs, that are not directly attributable to the     ䷅頇䋧錄䡝榟榣⺭鞳劲鞳榣ꝴ䫚䟕⛯
   acquisition, construction or production of qualifying assets,      ╯䢦僗⠺堑䡗儗亦榣榟僿ꝴ氳䮇沠╈
   are recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they       澭鏂
   are incurred.

   Taxation                                                           爀ꯁ
   Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently         䢦䐃爀ꝧ䶺䧘儗僿䟕♀爀ꯁ⹖鸚䋟爀ꯁ
   payable and deferred tax.                                          ╯竬⽰

   The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the       儗僿䟕♀爀ꯁ╤劯䴆儗䇗䈲䟕鐑爀嶯
   year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax because of     ⮵銷畁㍕亦⪭☾䇗䈲䟕鐑爀䡝⺎䣩峎
   items of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in       ╯䷅䶺ꯁ泘⹖姧鸞夯ꯄ鐑爀䡝┘⺎䣩
   other years and items that are never taxable or deductible.        峎╯ꯁ泘䟕鐑爀嶯⮵艃ꢝ爀⯥嶯⮵僗
   The Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax      䢦┘⺱儗꥘㎯╯儗僿爀ꯁ靷⥍╤䧞㕕
   rates that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the       ⼔僿儕⯥䄖⯆㲋䡝㴨韭┕䄖⯆㲋╯爀
   end of the reporting period.                                       曎銷畁

   Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                儗⪝⺚╤㶩禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑╈鞳榣⹖靷
   between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the      ⥍╯韰ꪫ⡭艃銷畁䟕鐑爀嶯⮵䢦榫注
   consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax        䟕爀㓹ꝴ╯倭侇䄐갯澭鏂鸚䋟爀ꯁ儗
   base used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax       ⪝⺚┉芙㶩䢦僗䟕鐑爀倭侇䄐갯澭鏂
   liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary     鸚䋟爀ꯁ靷⥍儗⪝⺚┉芙㶩䢦僗⺎䣩
   differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for      峎倭侇䄐갯澭鏂鸚䋟爀ꯁ鞳榣䘞♓⺎
   all deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is      耉榫⛼䥡ꊷ䟕鐑爀嶯⮵╯⺎䣩峎倭侇
   probable that taxable profits will be available against which      䄐갯憡꡿⠭倭侇䄐갯榣榟舅⮤塠澭鏂
   those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such       哅 ⳉ ⺭ ✡ ꢝ 㛛┉ ꯁ ┘ 僎 䏆 ꮷ 䟕 鐑 爀
   deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the      嶯⮵◱┘僎䏆ꮷ僎銷嶯⮵╯◬佄╈╯
   temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other    鞳榣䡝靷⥍┦亦◬佄侇┾扝榣榟⺱璣
   than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in       䟕鐑爀⹖⺎䥡䣩倭侇䓪䄐갯⯞鎊璣鸚
   a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the      䋟 爀 ꯁ 鞳 榣 ⹖ 靷 ⥍ ┘ ◀ 澭 鏂塝 㛛⠭
   accounting profit and at the time of the transaction does          り闌⮤塠澭鏂榣榟倭侇䄐갯⯞┘澭鏂
   not give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary            鸚䋟爀ꯁ靷⥍
   differences. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not
   recognised if the temporary differences arises from the initial
   recognition of goodwill.

   Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary       㶩艃亦ꡭ㸋⪝⺚╯䤸鞳僗꟯╯䟕鐑爀倭
   differences associated with investments in subsidiaries,            侇䄐갯澭鏂鸚䋟爀ꯁ靷⥍䘞⠭萵儗꥘
   except where the Group is able to control the reversal of the       ㎯耉㛭䫞⯆倭侇䄐갯╯䲡㍑⹖倭侇䄐갯
   temporary difference and it is probable that the temporary          䏭僗⺎耉亦⺎釤㵩✿┘僎䲡㍑⯞ꢝ㛛
   difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred     艃僗꟯䤸鞳僗꟯╯⺎䣩峎倭侇䄐갯榣榟
   tax assets arising from deductible temporary differences            ╯鸚䋟爀ꯁ鞳榣⥪亦䏭僗⺎耉㵩僗⩘餉
   associated with such investments are only recognised to             䟕鐑爀嶯⮵䥡ꊷ⺎Ⳃ榫倭侇䄐갯╯⮵沠
   the extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient        ⹖䏘璣ꯏ僿亦⺎釤㵩✿䲡㍑侇澭鏂
   taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of the
   temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in
   the foreseeable future.

   The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the       鸚䋟爀ꯁ鞳榣╯韰ꪫ⡭亦㕕⼔僿儕◀
   end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that it       ♓囸鋁┾♓┘⫋⺎耉暢䐃餉㛭䟕鐑爀
   is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be       嶯⮵♓䷅㍑鼨䡝鼨⮇鞳榣憡꡿◀♓
   available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.        䣩峎

   Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax         鸚䋟爀ꯁ鞳榣⹖靷⥍╤䧞ꯏ僿亦⨑麅靷
   rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the         ⥍䡝闭杯鞳榣僿ꝴ⪐䟕榫╯爀曎◀♓銷
   liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate    ꄈ縡爀曎╤㓹亦㕕⼔僿儕⯥䄖⯆㲋䡝
   (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively              㴨韭┕䄖⯆㲋╯爀曎⹖爀嫏
                                                                                    
   enacted by the end of the reporting period.

   The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets              鸚䋟爀ꯁ靷⥍⹖鞳榣╯銷ꄈ⹛佖儗꥘㎯
   reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the            ꯏ僿亦㕕⼔僿儕䷅㍑䡝⨑麅⪭鞳榣⹖靷
   manner in which the Group expects, at the end of the                ⥍╯韰ꪫ⡭䏳╯爀ꯁ䏳冽
   reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount of
   its assets and liabilities.

   Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there           ⠭僗⺎❤嫏䍛⯆㓵车垷⮵㵩儗僿爀ꯁ鞳
   is a legally enforceable right to set off current tax assets        榣艃儗僿爀ꯁ靷⥍䥡ꊷ┦僗꟯鞳榣艃
   against current tax liabilities and when they relate to income      靷⥍艃⺱┉爀ⳉ嚂꟯⺸⺱┉䟕鐑爀㴨냇
   taxes levied to the same taxable entity by the same taxation        䐹䷅╯䢦䐃爀僗꟯侇鸚䋟爀ꯁ鞳榣艃
   authority.                                                          靷⥍⺎◎注䥡ꊷ

   Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss,          儗僿⹖鸚䋟爀ꯁ╤亦䮇沠⪐澭鏂䘞⠭
   except when it relates to items that are recognised in other        萵⪭艃亦⪭☾ꪫ䷅沠⪐䡝泣䫚亦垷沠
   comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case,          ⪐澭鏂╯ꯁ泘僗꟯⯞ꢝ㛛亦塝䗯媲┖
   the current and deferred tax are also recognised in other           儗僿⹖鸚䋟爀ꯁ◱⮇⮯亦⪭☾ꪫ䷅沠
   comprehensive income or directly in equity, respectively.           䡝泣䫚亦垷沠⪐澭鏂㶩㍕㵴哅ⳉ⺭✡
   Where current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial           鶡车⮤塠僎銷谊杼縡榣榟╯儗僿爀ꯁ䡝
   accounting for a business combination, the tax effect is            鸚䋟爀ꯁ縡銨爀ⳉ䏆ꮷ╤銷⪌哅ⳉ⺭
   included in the accounting for the business combination.            ✡╯僎銷谊杼⪐

   In assessing any uncertainty over income tax treatments,           亦鍑⚽䢦䐃爀谊杼╯┘澭㲋䓪侇儗꥘
   the Group considers whether it is probable that the relevant       ㎯縓䜢注꟯爀ⳉ嚂꟯佭⻖⺎耉䫚⹨⠕
   tax authority will accept the uncertain tax treatment used,        ⮯꥘㎯㴨냇㏇⪭䢦䐃爀榼㕕╈✳榫䡝
   or proposed to be use by individual group entities in their        䴨 ✳ 榫 氳 ┘ 澭 㲋 爀 ⳉ 谊 杼㞞 冽 ⺎ 耉
   income tax filings. If it is probable, the current and deferred    儗僿⹖鸚䋟爀ꯁꄊ㲋艃䢦䐃爀榼㕕╈
   taxes are determined consistently with the tax treatment in        氳爀ⳉ谊杼┉舜⠭注꟯爀ⳉ嚂꟯┘㜁
   the income tax filings. If it is not probable that the relevant    ⺎耉䫚⹨┘澭㲋氳爀ⳉ谊杼⯞✳榫僅
   taxation authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment,         ⺎耉氳ꄌ갯䡝ꯏ僿⡭✿⹛佖夷⠕┘澭㲋
   the effect of each uncertainty is reflected by using either the    䓪氳䏆ꮷ
   most likely amount or the expected value.

   Impairment losses on property and equipment and right-             擻哅⹖錄⤔♓⹖✳榫垷鞳榣╯峎⡭谱
   of-use assets                                                      䮇
   At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the          亦㕕⼔僿儕儗꥘㎯僎囸鋁⪭擻哅⹖錄
   carrying amounts of its property and equipment and right-          ⤔♓⹖僗僗꡿⺎✳榫䇗僿╯✳榫垷
   of-use assets with finite useful lives to determine whether        鞳榣╯韰ꪫ⡭♓ꄊ㲋佭⻖僗饋霆걦炐
   there is any indication that these assets have suffered an         鎊璣鞳榣䄖⭴杯峎⡭谱䮇⠭萵㰇㏇♴
   impairment loss. If any such indication exists, the recoverable    ⛰注꟯饋霆⯞僎⚽銷注꟯鞳榣╯⺎䷅
   amount of the relevant asset is estimated in order to              ㍑ꄌ갯♓ꄊ㲋峎⡭谱䮇㞞僗╯䈲
   determine the extent of the impairment loss (if any).

   The recoverable amount of property and equipment and               擻哅⹖錄⤔♓⹖✳榫垷鞳榣╯⺎䷅㍑ꄌ
   right-of-use assets are estimated individually, when it is not     갯╤⮇⮯⚽銷⠭扝嫏⮇⮯⚽銷鞳榣╯
   possible to estimate the recoverable amount of an asset            ⺎䷅㍑ꄌ갯⯞儗꥘㎯⚽銷鎊ꯁ鞳榣䢦
   individually, the Group estimates the recoverable amount of        㸋╯杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤╯⺎䷅㍑ꄌ갯
   the cash-generating unit to which the asset belongs.

   Impairment losses on property and equipment and right-of-           擻哅⹖錄⤔♓⹖✳榫垷鞳榣╯峎⡭谱
   use assets (Continued)                                              䮇箜
   If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a cash-generating         ⠭┉ꯁ鞳榣䡝杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤╯⺎䷅㍑
   unit) is estimated to be less than its carrying amount, the         ꄌ갯⚽銷⛧亦⪭韰ꪫ⡭⯞鎊鞳榣䡝杯
   carrying amount of the asset (or a cash-generating unit)            ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤╯韰ꪫ⡭䣩峎舘⪭⺎䷅㍑
   is reduced to its recoverable amount. For corporate assets          ꄌ갯榺亦⚀哅鞳榣䡝鼨⮇⚀哅鞳榣┘
   or portion of corporate assets which cannot be allocated            耉⺭杼┉舜㏑⮇鿦舘┉⠕杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤
   on a reasonable and consistent basis to a cash-generating           ⪐儗꥘㎯奄鬴┉磞杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤氳韰
   unit, the Group compares the carrying amount of a group of          ꪫ⡭ⴎ䦮⚀哅鞳榣韰ꪫ⡭䡝鼨⮇䄖⮇
   cash-generating units, including the carrying amounts of the        鿦舘鎊磞杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤氳⚀哅鞳榣韰ꪫ
   corporate assets or portion of corporate assets allocated           ⡭艃鎊磞杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤╯⺎䷅㍑ꄌ갯
   to that group of cash-generating units, with the recoverable        ⮇鿦峎⡭谱䮇侇鎊峎⡭谱䮇꺙⩝⮇鿦
   amount of the group of cash-generating units. In allocating         ♓꡺⛧♴⛰り闌氳韰ꪫ⡭⠭鸸榫縡
   the impairment loss, the impairment loss is allocated first         䏳㓹亦鎊ㄲ⛤⪐䡝鎊磞杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤
   to reduce the carrying amount of any goodwill (if applicable)       ⺨ꯁ鞳榣╯韰ꪫ⡭䧞奄❆⮇鿦舘⪭☾鞳
   and then to the other assets on a pro-rata basis based on           榣┉ꯁ鞳榣氳韰ꪫ⡭┘僎峎舘⛧亦⪭
   the carrying amount of each asset in the unit or the group          ⪝䇖⡭峎⭴す䡗儗㞞⺎銷ꄈ⪭✳榫
   of cash-generating units. The carrying amount of an asset           ⧎⡭㞞⺎ꄊ㲋⹖ꦼ樧╈氳僅냖縖儗
   is not reduced below the highest of its fair value less costs       㵩⮇鿦舘鎊鞳榣氳峎⡭谱䮇╯ꄌ갯䧞奄
   of disposal (if measurable), its value in use (if determinable)     ❆⮇鿦舘鎊ㄲ⛤䡝鎊磞杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤氳
   and zero. The amount of the impairment loss that would              ⪭☾鞳榣峎⡭谱䮇ⷊ侇亦䮇沠⪐澭鏂
   otherwise have been allocated to the asset is allocated
   pro rata to the other assets of the unit or the group of
   cash-generating units. An impairment loss is recognised
   immediately in profit or loss.

   Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the carrying        ⠭ 峎 ⡭ 谱 䮇 ⪭ 䏳 䲡 ㍑⯞ 鎊 鞳 榣䡝 杯
   amount of the asset (or a cash-generating unit or a group of        ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤䡝┉磞杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤╯韰
   cash-generating units) is increased to the revised estimate         ꪫ⡭ꯄ㘎ⱶ舘⪭祺⟣銬╯⚽銷⺎䷅㍑ꄌ
   of its recoverable amount, but so that the increased carrying       갯䘞㘎ⱶ䏳╯韰ꪫ⡭丩갯┘䐃飃鷪⠭
   amount does not exceed the carrying amount that would have          萵亦鷪䏢䇗䈲儔僗㶩鎊ꯁ鞳榣䡝杯ꄌ
   been determined had no impairment loss been recognised              榣榟ㄲ⛤䡝┉磞杯ꄌ榣榟ㄲ⛤澭鏂峎
   for the asset (or a cash-generating unit or a group of cash-        ⡭谱䮇╯⸊僗韰ꪫ⡭峎⡭谱䮇䲡㍑ⷊ
   generating units) in prior years. A reversal of an impairment       侇亦䮇沠澭鏂
   loss is recognised immediately in profit or loss.

   Financial instruments                                               ꄌ踅䄄
   Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised on the    樧꥘㎯㴨냇䡗憡䄄⺭硝卋乄氳┉⺲銬
   consolidated statement of financial position when a group           硝亡侇儗⪝⺚僎㏇禍⺭靸ⳉ敻媲辑┕
   entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the         澭鏂ꄌ踅鞳榣⹖ꄌ踅靷⥍䢦僗♓䅻釨
   instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of financial         亡䌋頇鞙䡝⭴す╯ꄌ踅鞳榣㏱䧞◬佄仼
   assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade date              㓹嶗鶡车澭鏂⹖䱨ꢝ澭鏂♓䅻釨亡䌋
   basis. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or sales        頇鞙䡝⭴す佭䧘頇鞙䡝⭴す釒姲䧞注꟯
   of financial assets that require delivery of assets within the      䄬㕙╈╯釨⯞䡝䜉❆錄㲋╯僿꡿⪐◬♀
   time frame established by regulation or convention in the           鞳榣╯ꄌ踅鞳榣
   market place.

   accordance with HKFRS 15. Transaction costs that are                 15谝銷ꄈ䷅頇䡝氪车ꄌ踅鞳榣⹖ꄌ踅

   or financial liabilities at fair value through profit or loss        靷⥍ꢝ㛛泣䫚䟕⛯╯◬佄䡗儗╤亦⮤
   (“FVTPL”)) are added to or deducted from the fair value of           塠澭鏂侇ⱶ⪌ꄌ踅鞳榣䡝ꄌ踅靷⥍氳

   the acquisition of financial assets or financial liabilities at      鵂鷪䮇沠䧞⪝䇖⡭⮛韰╯ꄌ踅鞳榣⹖ꄌ
   FVTPL are recognised immediately in profit or loss.                  踅靷⥍泣䫚䟕⛯╯◬佄䡗儗ⷊ侇亦䮇沠
                                                                        ⪐澭鏂

   The effective interest method is a method of calculating             㴨꤀⮵曎嫏憡銷畁┉ꯁꄌ踅鞳榣䡝ꄌ踅
   the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability       靷⥍╯䶥ꊷ䡗儗⹖䧞僗꟯僿ꝴ⮇鿦⮵䕜
   and of allocating interest income and interest expense over          ䷅⪌⹖⮵䕜鞞榫╯亡嫏㴨꤀⮵曎憡䧞
   the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate         ꄌ踅鞳榣䡝ꄌ踅靷⥍╯ꯏ僿䇗僿䡝㞞
   that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and            鸸榫鬴湿僿ꝴ㵩⚽銷儔✿杯ꄌ䷅⪌⹖
   payments (including all fees and points paid or received             ♀堑ⴎ䦮䄖䶺♀䡝䷅⹦╯啶䡗㴨꤀⮵
   that form an integral part of the effective interest rate,           曎╯┉鼨⮇╯䢦僗鞞榫⹖鞞曎◬佄䡗
   transaction costs and other premiums or discounts) through           儗⹖⪭☾嶯⧎䡝䤽阁嶗澭鞟杯舘亦⮤
   the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,     塠澭鏂侇╯韰ꪫ屻⡭╯奄曎
   or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying
   amount on initial recognition.

   Interest income which are derived from the Group’s ordinary          ㏇儗꥘㎯仼䅻哅ⳉ鷪╈暢⹦氳⮵䕜䷅
   course of business are presented as revenue.                         ⪌⮛憡䷅⪌

   assets

   Financial assets that meet the following conditions are              瑟⺭┖⮛卋♭╯ꄌ踅鞳榣⪭䏳䧞䶥ꊷ䡗
   subsequently measured at amortised cost:                             儗銷ꄈ

   •   the financial asset is held within a business model whose        •   哅ⳉ嘘䌋氳泘氳憡䧏僗ꄌ踅鞳榣♓
       objective is to collect contractual cash flows; and                  ䷅⹦⺭硝杯ꄌ孲ꄈ⹖

   •   the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to cash       •   ⺭硝卋堑釨㲋㏇䧘㲋仼僿侇僎榣榟
       flows that are solely payments of principal and interest             杯ꄌ孲ꄈ縡鎊璣杯ꄌ孲ꄈ⥪榫⛼
       on the principal amount outstanding.                                 䶺♀儗ꄌ⹖㶉儔⨑麅儗ꄌ╯⮵䕜

   Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all             僿ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇䧘注꟯䄄亦ꯏ僿䇗
   possible default events over the expected life of the relevant        僿⪐䢦僗⺎耉氳鸂硝◄♭㵩榣榟╯ꯏ僿
   instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents          ⟔ 鞚 谱 䮇注 ⹛1 2 ⠕ 僖 ꯏ 僿 ⟔ 鞚 谱 䮇
   the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from          ր 1 2 ⠕ 僖 ꯏ 僿 ⟔ 鞚 谱 䮇 ց⯞ 䧘 ꯏ 僿
   default events that are possible within 12 months after the           亦㕕⼔仼僿䏳12⠕僖⪐⺎耉氪榟氳鸂
   reporting date. Assessments are done based on the Group’s             硝◄♭㵷舜氳鼨♷僿ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇
   historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors that are      鍑⚽╤劯䴆儗꥘㎯氳塽⺒⟔鞚谱䮇祺꿨
   specific to the debtors, general economic conditions and an           鶡车┾劯䴆⥍ⳉ☕攑僗氳㍕碜┉芙祺
   assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting            忴敻媲♓⹖㵴㕕⼔仼僿樧⯥敻媲氳鍑⚽
   date as well as the forecast of future conditions.                    ♓⹖㵴儔✿敻媲氳ꯏ峮⛼⭴鐪丞

   The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for accounts                儗꥘㎯┉鞉僎㵴꺞靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮15
   receivable that result from transactions within the scope            谝疘㎠⪐◬佄䢦榣榟氳䟕䷅韰堑氳僿
   of HKFRS 15 and the ECL on these assets are assessed                 ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇鶡车澭鏂┾僎ꄡ㵴僗
   individually for debtors with significant balances and/or            갯磶埞⥍ⳉ☕氳鞳榣⠕⮯鶡车ꯏ僿⟔鞚
   collectively using a provision matrix with debtors having            谱䮇鍑⚽⹖ҧ䡝ꄡ㵴僗⛍⟔鞚鍑碍
   similar credit ratings.                                              氳⥍ⳉ☕✳榫䬠⮛ꢛ鶡车꥘냇ꯏ僿⟔
                                                                        鞚谱䮇鍑⚽

   For all other instruments, the Group applies the general             㵴亦䢦僗⪭☾䄄儗꥘㎯僎䫑榫┉芙
   approach to measure ECL for all financial assets, if any,            䓪氳亡嫏✿銷ꄈ䢦僗ꄌ踅鞳榣氳ꯏ僿
   which are subject to impairment under HKFRS 9. On this               ⟔ 鞚 谱 䮇㞞 僗┾ ꯄ 劯 䴆 꺞  靸 ⳉ 㕕
   basis, the Group measures the loss allowance equal to 12m            ⼔ 嶗 ⯞ 瑮 9 谝 鶡 车 峎 ⡭㏇ ♓ 塝 憡 㓹 灅
   ECL, unless there has been a significant increase in credit risk     䗯媲┖儗꥘㎯僎♓12⠕僖ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱
   since initial recognition, in which case the Group recognises        䮇✿銷ꄈ谱䮇䲡⤔ꢝꪞ㏇꺙埠澭鏂䏳
   lifetime ECL.                                                        ⟔鞚겨ꤠ㛻䆋㘎ⱶ㏇塝䗯媲┖儗꥘㎯
                                                                        僎澭鏂僿ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇

   (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                            (i)   ⟔鞚겨ꤠ㛻䆋㘎ⱶ
         In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                   憡鍑⚽⟔鞚겨ꤠ㏇꺙埠澭鏂䏳僗⻖
         significantly since initial recognition, the Group compares          㛻䆋㘎ⱶ儗꥘㎯僎㵩ꄌ踅䄄㏇
         the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument          㕕⼔仼氪榟氳鸂硝겨ꤠ艃ꄌ踅䄄
         as at the reporting date with the risk of a default                  ㏇꺙埠澭鏂仼氪榟氳鸂硝겨ꤠ注
         occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of              奄鬴儗꥘㎯鶡车鍑⚽侇僎縓䜢⺭
         initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group            杼┾僗⩘⮇䶺䧏氳㲋ꄈ⹖㲋䓪鞳乢
         considers both quantitative and qualitative information, if          㞞 鸸 榫ⴎ 䦮 ⹉ 縓 鷪 䏢 祺 꿨 ⹖ 扝
         applicable, that is reasonable and supportable, including            ꯄ縶鞞륛㛻☕ⱱ擻ⱱ縡⹦䐃氳⯥渥
         historical experience and forward-looking information                䓪⟔䕜  
         that is available without undue cost or effort.

        For internal credit risk management, the Group considers            㶩⪐鼨⟔鞚겨ꤠ畚杼縡銨⠭⪐鼨

   (iv) Write-off policy                                               (iv) 䱨ꊷ䷓瓁
        The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                樧僗鞳乢걦炐㵴䣆亡谊亦㌅ꄆ靸ⳉ
        information indicating that the counterparty is in severe           ㍮ꦙ⹖扝㴨꤀䷅㍑⺎耉❆㞞㵴䣆

        been placed under liquidation or has entered into                   縓䜢嫏䏱䙬釤䏳⠭⺭鸸
                                                                                       鹂䱨ꊷ
        bankruptcy proceedings. Financial assets written off                氳ꄌ踅鞳榣⺎耉☱ꯄ䧞儗꥘㎯䷅㍑

        the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account                磚塛澭鏂◄♭♴⛰⪭䏳䷅㍑㏇䮇
        legal advice where appropriate. A write-off constitutes             沠╈澭鏂
        a derecognition event. Any subsequent recoveries are
        recognised in profit or loss.

   (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v) ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇╯銷ꄈ⹖澭鏂
       The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability             ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇╯銷ꄈ憡鸂硝哯曎
       of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the           鸂硝谱䮇曎ⷊ鸂硝谱䮇䈲⹖鸂
       loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The        硝겨ꤠ╯⭺丩鸂硝哯曎⹖鸂硝谱
       assessment of the probability of default and loss given             䮇曎╯鍑⚽╤㓹亦塽⺒丩䴆⹖⯥渥
       default are based on historical data and forward-looking            䓪⟔䕜ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇氳ꯏ⚽╤扝
       information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and             ⢏哯曎ⱶ垷䇖㏱ꄌ갯♓垷ꄆ澭㲋
       probability-weighted amount that is determined with the             氪榟鸂硝氳겨ꤠ
       respective risks of default occurring as the weights.

       Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference between         ┉芙縡銨ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇╤劯䴆⺭
       all contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in           硝䟕♀儗꥘㎯╯䢦僗⺭硝杯ꄌ孲ꄈ
       accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that          艃儗꥘㎯ꯏ僿䷅⹦╯䢦僗杯ꄌ孲ꄈ
       the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective         ╯ꝴ氳䄐갯䧞⮤㡐澭鏂侇ꄊ㲋╯
       interest rate determined at initial recognition.                  㴨꤀⮵曎鞟杯⛼⭴⚽銷

   Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                       ꄌ踅鞳榣峎⡭箜

       For collective assessment, the Group takes into                  㶩꥘냇鍑⚽縡銨儗꥘㎯㵩亦⯆㲋
       consideration the following characteristics when                 磞⮯侇縓䜢♓┖攑䐹
       formulating the grouping:

       •   Past-due status;                                           •   鷀僿敻媲

       •   Nature, size, background and industry of debtors;          •   ⥍ⳉ☕╯䓪韭釨嘘羞俍⹖车
           and                                                            哅⹖

       •   External credit ratings where available.                   •   ⺎榫╯㛛鼨⟔鞚鍑碍

       The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to            畚杼㷼㲋僿囸鋁⮇磞亡嫏♓澭⟊
       ensure the constituents of each group continue to share        ⺨磞⮯╯磞䡗ꯁ泘筷箜僗注⛍╯
       similar credit risk characteristics.                           ⟔鞚겨ꤠ攑䐹

       Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying      ⮵䕜䷅⪌╤劯䴆ꄌ踅鞳榣╯韰ꪫ竬
       amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset       ⡭銷畁ꢝꪞꄌ踅鞳榣⛼⭴⟔鞚峎
       is credit impaired, in which case interest income is           ⡭㏇ 塝 䗯 媲 ┖⮵ 䕜 ䷅ ⪌ 䧞 舅 ┖
       calculated based on amortised cost of the financial            ┉㕕⼔僿ꝧ㡐╯ꄌ踅鞳榣╯䶥ꊷ
       asset from the beginning of the next reporting period.         䡗儗銷畁

       The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in             儗꥘㎯鵂鷪鐪丞韰ꪫ⡭亦䮇沠╈
       profit or loss for all financial instruments by adjusting      㶩䢦僗ꄌ踅䄄澭鏂峎⡭䷅沠䡝
       their carrying amount, with the exception of accounts          谱䮇䘞䟕䷅僗䥡䥨㰃㷤㲙䢒韰堑
       receivable from secured margin clients and loans               ♓⹖鞚堑⹖㗭堑ꢝ㛛⪭注䟕鐪丞
       and advances where the corresponding adjustment is             鵂鷪谱䮇䲡⤔韰澭鏂
       recognised through a loss allowance account.

       In assessing the ECL for financial assets, the Group           ㏇鍑⚽ꄌ踅鞳榣氳ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇
       performs the assessment based on the Group’s historical        侇儗꥘㎯劯䴆儗꥘㎯鷪䏢氳⟔鞚
       credit loss experience, adjusted for factors specific to       谱䮇祺꿨鶡车鍑⚽┾䧞拨㕕⼔樧
       the borrowers and the current conditions at the reporting      仼⠺堑☕氳攑㲋㍕碜⹖樧侇敻媲
       date as well as the forecast of future conditions. The         ♓⹖儔✿敻媲氳ꯏ峮⛼⭴鐪丞儗
       Group also reviews the value of collateral received            ꥘㎯◱僎囸鋁舅㲙䢒䷅⹦氳䥡䥨
       from the customers in measuring impairment. The                ⿁⧎⡭♓銷ꄈ峎⡭⚽銷峎⡭ꄌ갯
       methodology and assumptions used for estimating the            氳亡嫏⹖⡾錄㵩⛼㲋僿鍑⚽♓峎
       impairment amount are reviewed regularly to reduce             㵼ꯏ僿谱䮇⹖㴨꤀谱䮇祺꿨╯ꝴ
       any differences between loss estimates and actual loss         ╯䄐樟
       experience.

   contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire, or        㷋幥䡝儗꥘㎯䄖⺸⺁┉㴨냇鮺燯ꄌ踅

   Financial liabilities and equity                                      ꄌ踅靷⥍⹖垷沠

   an equity instrument.

   Equity instruments                                                    垷沠䄄

   interest in the assets of an entity after deducting all of its        靷⥍䏳╯Ⱂ궠垷沠╯♴⛰⺭硝儗⪝⺚
   liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the Company are             䢦氪车╯罇儗䄄䧞䄖䷅䢦䐃堑ꯁ䣩ꢝ
   recognised at the proceeds received, net of direct issue              泣䫚氪车䡗儗䏳澭鏂
   costs.

   For financial liabilities that are designated as at FVTPL, the          㶩䧘㲋憡鵂鷪䮇沠䧞⪝䇖⡭⮛韰╯ꄌ踅
   amount of changes in the fair value of the financial liability          靷⥍縡銨㍕ꄌ踅靷⥍╯⟔鞚겨ꤠ闭Ⳃ
   that is attributable to changes in the credit risk of that liability    縡㵷舜╯鎊靷⥍⪝䇖⡭氳闭Ⳃ丩갯亦⪭
   is recognised in other comprehensive income, unless the                 ☾ꪫ䷅沠澭鏂ꢝꪞ亦⪭☾ꪫ䷅沠
   recognition of the effects of changes in the liability’s credit         澭鏂鎊靷⥍╯⟔鞚겨ꤠ闭Ⳃ╯䏆ꮷ㵩榣
   risk in other comprehensive income would create or enlarge              榟䡝䴺㛻䮇沠╯僎銷ꎭ鿦⯞⛼⮯鑝鵂
   an accounting mismatch in profit or loss. Financial liabilities         鷪䮇沠䧞⪝䇖⡭⮛韰╯ꄌ踅靷⥍╤䧞⪝
   at FVTPL are measured at fair value, with any gains or losses           䇖⡭銷ꄈ縡ꄆ亏銷ꄈ榣榟氳♴⛰䷅沠
   arising on remeasurement recognized in profit or loss. Fair             䡝谱䮇亦䮇沠澭鏂⪝䇖⡭䧞ꡭ錝39䢦
   value is determined in the manner described in note 39.                 鳯氳亡䌋ꄊ㲋

   Derecognition of financial liabilities                                  䱨ꢝ澭鏂ꄌ踅靷⥍
   The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and only             樧┦⥪樧儗꥘㎯氳鞊♴暢鉮ꢝ⹦寕䡝
   when, the Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or              㷋幥侇儗꥘㎯䱨ꢝ澭鏂ꄌ踅靷⥍䱨ꢝ
   have expired. The difference between the carrying amount                澭鏂╯ꄌ踅靷⥍╯韰ꪫ⡭艃䄖♀⹖䟕♀
   of the financial liability derecognised and the consideration           ♏⧎ꝴ╯䄐갯╤亦䮇沠╈澭鏂
   paid and payable is recognised in profit or loss.

   Financial assets and financial liabilities offsetting                   ꄌ踅鞳榣艃ꄌ踅靷⥍╯䥡ꊷ
   A financial asset and a financial liability are offset and the          樧䡝⥪樧儗꥘㎯泘⯥僗嫏䏱┕⺎䍛
   net amount is presented in the consolidated statement of                ⯆㓵车氳垷⮵♓䥡ꊷ䄖澭鏂ꄌ갯⹖䴨
   financial position when, and only when, the Group currently             ♓屻갯㓹嶗磶畁䡝㏇闭杯鞳榣侇⺱侇
   has a legally enforceable right to set off the recognised               磶岳靷⥍ꄌ踅鞳榣艃ꄌ踅靷⥍⺎◎注
   amounts; and intends either to settle on a net basis, or to             䥡ꊷ┾亦禍⺭靸ⳉ敻媲辑⪐⮛屻갯
   realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously.

4. Critical Accounting Judgements and Key                             4. ꄆ㛻僎銷⮮亝⹖⚽銷┘伺僮㍕
   Sources of Estimation Uncertainty                                     碜氳╚釒✿嶏
   In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, the          亦䟕榫儗꥘㎯僎銷䷓瓁侇儗⪝⺚蝕◄
   directors of the Company are required to make judgements,           ꯄ㶩儔耉亦⪭☾✿嶏⹦䐃氳鞳榣⹖靷⥍
   estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts of             ╯韰ꪫ⡭⛼⭴⮮亝⚽銷⹖⡾錄
   assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other
   sources.

   The estimates and associated assumptions are based on               ⚽銷⹖注꟯⡾錄╤劯䴆鷪䏢祺꿨⹖⪭☾
   historical experience and other factors that are considered to      逈鏂憡㸋注꟯╯㍕碜⛼⭴㴨꤀磶冽⺎
   be relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.        耉艃鎊璣⚽銷僗樟

   The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on            儗꥘㎯僎䧏箜囸鋁鎊璣⚽銷⹖注꟯⡾
   an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are            錄⠭僎銷⚽銷╯⟣銬⥪䏆ꮷ⟣銬⚽銷
   recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if        氳僿ꝴ⯞僗꟯⚽銷⟣銬㵩亦⛼⭴⟣銬
   the revision affects only that period or in the period of the       ╯僿ꝴ澭鏂䡝⠭⟣銬㵴儗僿⹖儔✿僿
   revision and future periods if the revision affects both current    ꝴ㏱僎啶䡗䏆ꮷ⯞僎亦⛼⭴⟣銬╯僿
   and future periods.                                                 ꝴ⹖儔✿僿ꝴ澭鏂

   Key sources of estimation uncertainty                               ⚽銷┘伺僮㍕碜╯╚釒✿嶏
   The following are the key assumptions concerning the future,        ♓┖憡亦㕕⼔僿儕⺎耉僗ꄆ㛻겨ꤠ㵷舜
   and other key sources of estimation uncertainty at the end          㶩┖⠕靸䷓䇗䈲鞳榣艃靷⥍韰ꪫ⡭⛼⭴
   of the reporting period that may have a significant risk of         ꄆ㛻鐪丞縡僗꟯仼䏳╯╚釒⡾錄⹖⚽銷
   causing a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of            ┘伺僮㍕碜╯⪭☾╚釒✿嶏
   assets and liabilities within the next financial year.

   The application of ECL Model                                        䟕榫ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇嘘䌋
   The directors of the Company estimate the amount of                 儗⪝⺚蝕◄劯䴆注꟯ꄌ踅䄄╯⟔鞚겨
   loss allowance for ECL on financial assets at amortised             ꤠ⚽銷䧞䶥ꊷ䡗儗⮛韰╯ꄌ踅鞳榣╯ꯏ
   cost based on the credit risk of the respective financial           僿⟔鞚谱䮇╯谱䮇䲡⤔ꄌ갯谱䮇䲡⤔
   instrument. The loss allowance amount is measured as                ꄌ갯╤䧞鞳榣韰ꪫ⡭艃⚽銷儔✿杯ꄌ
   the difference between the asset’s carrying amount and              孲ꄈ杯⡭╯ꝴ䄐갯銷ꄈ┾縓䜢⺨ꄌ踅
   the present value of estimated future cash flows with the           䄄╯ꯏ僿儔✿⟔鞚谱䮇㵴⺨ꯁꄌ踅
   consideration of expected future credit loss of the respective      䄄╯⟔鞚겨ꤠ⹖ꯏ僿杯ꄌ孲ꄈ䢦⛼
   financial instrument. The assessment of the credit risk and         鍑⚽寘⹖냖䈲⚽銷⹖┘澭㲋䓪ꯏ僿⟔
   expected cash flows of the respective financial instrument          鞚谱䮇䲡⤔㵴⚽銷╯闭Ⳃ䷨䚋亦ꄊ㲋
   involves a high degree of estimation and uncertainty. The           峎⡭䲡⤔侇⚽銷㵩ⴎ䦮儔✿杯ꄌ孲ꄈ
   provision of ECL is sensitive to changes in estimates. The          ⹖䥡䥨⿁⧎⡭氳ꄌ갯⹖侇ꝴ⠭㴨꤀儔
   estimates would include the amount and timing of future             ✿杯ꄌ孲ꄈ⛧亦ꯏ僿䡝飃⭴ꯏ僿⯞⺎
   cash flows and collateral value when determining impairment         耉⭴杯ꄆ㛻峎⡭谱䮇䡝ꄆ㛻峎⡭谱䮇
   allowances. When the actual future cash flows are less than         䲡㍑僗꟯䧞䶥ꊷ䡗儗⮛韰╯ꄌ踅鞳榣
   expected or more than expected, a material impairment               ╯ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇鞳乢亦禍⺭靸ⳉ㕕辑ꡭ
   loss or a material reversal of impairment loss may arise,           錝39䥑꨾
   accordingly. The information about the ECL on financial
   assets at amortised cost are disclosed in note 39 to the
   consolidated financial statements.

   The Risk Management Department is responsible for                   겨ꤠ畚杼鼨靷鞊⯆㲋⹖禵䧏ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱
   developing and maintaining the processes for measuring ECL          䮇╯銷ꄈ䈌ⴎ䦮泃㴕⟔鞚겨ꤠ硱⪌
   including monitoring of credit risk, incorporation of forward-      ⯥渥䓪鞳乢⹖銷ꄈꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇䢦榫亡
   looking information and the method used to measure ECL,             嫏┾澭⟊儗꥘㎯銬僗䷓瓁⹖䈌♓鸸
   and ensuring that the Group has policies and procedures in          樧禵闀⹖꿨镜榫亦鍑⚽⹖銷ꄈꯏ僿⟔鞚
   place to appropriately maintain and validate models used to         谱䮇╯嘘㑔
   assess and measure ECL.

4. Critical Accounting Judgements and Key                            4. ꄆ㛻僎銷⮮亝⹖⚽銷┘伺僮㍕
   Sources of Estimation Uncertainty (Continued)                        碜氳╚釒✿嶏箜
   Key sources of estimation uncertainty (Continued)                    ⚽銷┘伺僮㍕碜╯╚釒✿嶏箜
   The application of ECL Model (Continued)                           䟕榫ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇嘘䌋箜
   Incorporation of forward-looking information                       硱⪌⯥渥䓪鞳乢
   The Group employs experts who use external and internal            儗꥘㎯纷鑂㵬㳄鷠榫㛛鼨⹖⪐鼨鞳乢
   information to generate scenarios of future forecasts of           䐃⭴注꟯祺忴闭ꄈ╯儔✿ꯏ峮敻媲䢦
   relevant economic variables. The internal and external             䫑榫⪐鼨⹖㛛鼨鞳乢ⴎ䦮儗꥘㎯塽⺒
   information used includes the historical data of the Group         丩䴆♓⹖⮇⮯榺䷓䈢嚂啶鞁䆸嚂꟯䡝
   and economic data and forecasts published by governmental          ⪭☾ꄌ踅嚂啶⪝⛜╯祺忴丩䴆⹖ꯏ峮
   bodies, monetary authorities or other financial institutions.      ㍕塝銷ꄈꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇侇儗꥘㎯㶩鍑
   Accordingly, when measuring ECL, the Group selects and             ⚽鹢䳢┾䫑榫夯ꯄ♀⭴┘䑒釒䡗儗䡝瞿
   uses reasonable and supportable forward-looking information        ⱱⷊ⺎暢䐃╯⺭杼⺎⛼憡❤䴆╯⯥渥䓪
   without undue cost or effort in its assessment, which is based     鞳乢塝╤㓹亦㵴┘⺱祺忴꿄Ⳃ㍕碜儔
   on assumptions and estimates for the future movement of            ✿闭Ⳃ╯⡾錄⹖⚽銷♓⹖鎊璣꿄Ⳃ㍕碜
   different economic drivers and how these drivers will affect       㞞⛰注◎䏆ꮷ⹖塽⺒鸂硝曎⹖㱴鈶祺忴
   each other as well as the correlation between historical           ㍕碜氳注꟯䓪畚杼㷼⚽銷鎊璣⯥渥䓪
   default rate and macro-economic factors. Management                祺忴㍕碜㞞┘⺱䗯媲┖氳㎜⪐榟榣竬
   estimates these forward-looking economic factors, such as          ⡭ 㘎 ꝏ㜊 哅 曎⮵ 曎 ⹖ 鵡 聽 璣
                                                                                             ┾ ⮇
   GDP growth, unemployment rate, interest rate and inflation         冫┘⺱ꄌ踅鞳榣鸂硝哯曎⹖鸂硝谱䮇
   etc. under different scenario and analyse the impact on            曎氳䏆ꮷ
   probability of default (“PD”) and loss given default (“LGD”)
   for different financial assets.

   Measurement of ECL                                                 銷ꄈꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇
   PD constitutes a key input in measuring ECL. PD is an              鸂硝哯曎啶䡗銷ꄈꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇╯꟯ꐷ
   estimate of the likelihood of default over a given time horizon    鮗⪌丩䴆鸂硝哯曎佭㵴┉㲋侇僿⪐鸂
   and the calculation is based on reasonable and appropriate         硝⺎耉䓪╯⚽銷┾䧞畚杼㷼鹢䳢⺭杼
   statistical rating models selected by the management. These        䕞樧╯礵銷鍑碍嘘㑔縡銷畁鎊璣礵銷
   statistical models are based on market data (where available),     嘘㑔㓹亦䄬㕙丩䴆㞞僗♓⹖ⴎ⻢㲋ꄈ
   as well as internal data comprising both quantitative and          ⹖㲋䓪㍕碜㞞鸸榫ⴎ䦮塽⺒丩䴆⹖儔
   qualitative factors, if applicable, which includes historical      ✿敻媲⡾錄艃ꯏ峮╯⪐鼨丩䴆畚杼㷼
   data, assumptions and expectations of future conditions. The       ䷅꥘僗꟯鞳乢┾鐪丞丩䴆♓⹛佖夯ꯄ♀
   management gathers this information and adjusts the data to        ⭴┘䑒釒䡗儗䡝瞿ⱱⷊ⺎暢䐃╯⺭杼⺎
   reflect probability-weighted forward looking information that      ⛼憡❤䴆╯哯曎ⱶ垷⯥渥䓪鞳乢
   is reasonable and supportable and available without undue
   cost or effort.

   LGD is an estimate of the loss arising on default. It is based     鸂硝谱䮇曎佭㵴鸂硝谱䮇氳⚽銷⪭㓹
   on the difference between the contractual cash flows due           亦⮽僿⺭硝杯ꄌ孲ꄈ艃鞚堑☕ꯏ僿䷅⮽
   and those that the lender would expect to receive, taking          氳⺭硝杯ꄌ孲ꄈ╯ꝴ氳䄐갯⺱侇僎縓
   into account cash flows from collateral and integral credit        䜢寘⹖⚽銷╯䥡䥨⿁榣榟氳杯ꄌ孲ꄈ⽰
   enhancements with estimation involved. The LGD models              丞냇⟔鞚㘎碍䄖䥡䥨鞳榣氳鸂硝谱䮇
   for secured assets consider forecasts of future collateral         曎嘘㑔縓䜢儔✿䥡䥨⿁⚽⡭氳ꯏ峮♓
   valuation taking into account sale discounts, transaction          ⹖䄖䥡䥨鞳榣╯ꊷす䤽䣩◬佄ꄈ⹖榼
   volume of the secured assets and seniority of claim. For           碠╯⨣⩝埠䈌㶩扝䥡䥨鞚堑縡銨鸂硝
   unsecured loans, the calculation of LGD includes the               谱䮇曎氳銷畁ⴎ䦮ꄊ㲋鸂硝䏳䷅㍑氳鞚
   judgments in determining the proportion of loan recovered          堑奄❆⹖䷅㍑僿꡿氳⮮亝
   after default and the duration of recovery.

   As explained in note 3, the Group monitors all financial              㞞ꡭ錝3䢦ꟹ鳯儗꥘㎯泃䫞ꯄ鹘㱥峎⡭

   clients are measured on lifetime ECL basis as for those               ⯞♓僿ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇㓹嶗銷ꄈ꺞
   credit risk had increased significantly since initial recognition.    靸ⳉ㕕⼔嶗⯞瑮9谝┾儔槡㲋⟔鞚겨ꤠ

   of an asset has significantly increased, the Group takes              ⹖⺎ꪥ氳㲋䓪⹖㲋ꄈ鞳乢㞞鸸榫ⴎ
   into account qualitative and quantitative information, if             䦮鷪䏢祺꿨⹖扝ꧏ♀⭴┘䑒釒䡗儗䡝ⲅ

   The Group entered into secured loan arrangements with                儗꥘㎯艃㲙䢒銬环䥡䥨鞚堑㱨䪶ⴎ⻢

   exercise these rights as power to direct the relevant activities     Ⳃꯄ縓䜢┉⮊◄㴨⹖䗯媲䫞⯆⸊⯞ⴎ

   from involvement with the pledged company; and (c) the               ⪝⺚氳垷ⱱ䏆ꮷ鞚堑☕氳㍑㕕ꄌ갯氳耉

   amount of the lender’s returns.

   nature of this business.

   The Group participates in both a defined contribution scheme        儗꥘㎯⺱侇⹉艃劯䴆꺞绹哅鴛⚔銷ⱁ

   a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”)                ⪝犉ꄌ銷ⱁ卋❆錄环╯䍛⯆䓪⪝犉ꄌ
   established under the Hong Kong Mandatory Provident Fund            銷ⱁր䍛犉ꄌ銷ⱁց 鎊璣銷ⱁ╯鞳榣
   Scheme Ordinance in December 2000. The assets of the                艃儗꥘㎯╯鞳榣⮇ꝧ┾♓暐环鋖畚☕
   schemes are held separately from those of the Group, in             畚杼╯㓹ꄌ䎬䌋䧏僗亦䍛犉ꄌ銷ⱁ錄
   funds under the control of independent trustees. Employees          环⯥㸋绹哅鴛⚔銷ⱁ䡗⿦╯⧀⿦㏱⺎鹢
   who were members of the ORSO Scheme prior to the                    䳢筷箜⹉ⱶ绹哅鴛⚔銷ⱁ䡝鮺憡⹉ⱶ䍛
   establishment of the MPF Scheme were offered a choice of            犉ꄌ銷ⱁ䘞亦2000䇗12僖1仼䡝♓䏳
   staying within the ORSO Scheme or switching to the MPF              䢦僗儗꥘㎯╯亏⪌绹⧀⿦㏱ꯄ⹉ⱶ䍛
   Scheme, whereas all new employees joining the Group on or           犉ꄌ銷ⱁ
   after 1 December 2000 are required to join the MPF Scheme.

37. Capital Risk Management                                              37. 鞳儗겨ꤠ畚杼
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in               儗 ꥘ ㎯ 畚 杼 ⪭ 鞳 儗♓ 澭 ⟊ 儗 ꥘ ㎯ ╯
   the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                  㴨냇㵩耉㛭筷箜䧞䧏箜祺揩㓹嶗祺
   while maximising the return to shareholders through the                揩⺱ 侇 鵂 鷪 ⨣ ⴫ ⥍ ⳉ ⹖ 垷 沠 磶 궠 㵩
   optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The capital               礢 ◀ 罇 典 ╯ ㍑ 㕕 僅 㛻 ⴫儗 ꥘ ㎯ ╯ 鞳
   structure of the Group consists of equity attributable to              儗几啶ⴎ䦮儗⪝⺚䳕僗☕䟕⛯垷沠
   owners of the Company, comprising issued share capital                 ⴎ 䦮 禍 ⺭ 靸 ⳉ 敻 媲 辑禍 ⺭ 垷 沠 闭 Ⳃ
   and reserves as set out on the consolidated statement of               辑⹖⺨舅ꡭ錝䢦鬼╯䄖氪车罇儗⹖⨶
   financial position, consolidated statement of changes in               ⤔儗 ꥘ ㎯ ╯ 丞 냇 瓁 樋 舅 ┕ 䇗  禵 䧏
   equity and respective notes. The Group’s overall strategy              ┘闭
   remains unchanged from the prior year.

   The management reviews the capital structure by considering             畚杼㷼鵂鷪縓䜢鞳儗䡗儗⹖罇儗注꟯겨
   the cost of capital and the risks associated with the share             ꤠ囸鋁鞳儗几啶僗ꗷ亦塝儗꥘㎯鵂鷪
   capital. In view of this, the Group manages its overall capital         䬠⹦⹖⨑麅ꉐ车⠺堑⹖氪车罇儗畚杼⪭
   structure through the drawdown and repayment of bank                    丞냇鞳儗几啶
   borrowings and issue of share capital.

   Several subsidiaries of the Group (the “Regulated Subsidiaries”)        儗 ꥘ ㎯ 萵 䇕 ꡭ 㸋 ⪝ ⺚ր⹨ 釨 畚 ꡭ 㸋 ⪝
   are registered with Securities and Futures Commission (“SFC”)           ⺚ ց㶩 䏘 璣 揩 鷠 ╯ 哅 ⳉ ⺸ 镜 ⯉ ⹖ 僿 鞁
   for the business they operate in. The Regulated Subsidiaries            ◄ⳉ泃㴕㡨⿦僎ր镜泃僎ցꯝ擀⹨釨
   are subject to liquid capital requirements under Securities and         畚ꡭ㸋⪝⺚ꯄ鹘㱥镜泃僎䢦䫑硱╯镜
   Futures (Financial Resources) Rules (“SF(FR)R”) adopted by              ⯉⹖僿鞁靸䷓鞳嶏釨⯞րSF(FR)Rց
   the SFC. Under the SF(FR)R, the Regulated Subsidiaries must             ╯ 孲 Ⳃ 鞳 儗 釨 㲋劯 䴆 S F ( F R ) R⹨ 釨
   maintain their liquid capital in excess of HK$3 million or 5% of        畚ꡭ㸋⪝⺚ꯄ禵䧏䏘璣╯孲Ⳃ鞳儗飃
   their total adjusted liabilities, whichever is higher. The required     鷪3,000,000⩕䡝䏘璣╯祺鐪丞靷⥍
   information is filed with SFC on a monthly basis.                       竬갯╯5%♓鬴냖縖憡嶗        䢦釨㲋鞳乢
                                                                           䧞僖⺸镜泃僎䬠◬

   Another subsidiary of the Group is licensed with the Insurance          儗꥘㎯⺁┉ꝴꡭ㸋⪝⺚䧏僗⟊ꤠ哅泃畚
   Authority. It is required to maintain a minimum net asset               㷀╯擀拨⪭ꯄ┉泣禵䧏僅⛧鞳榣屻⡭
   value of HK$100,000 at all times.                                       100,000⩕

   Market risk                                                       䄬㕙겨ꤠ
   Currency risk                                                     鞁䆸겨ꤠ
   Currency risk is the risk of loss due to adverse movements        鞁䆸겨ꤠ憡㍕艃䟕䷅⹖䟕♀◀㗟㛛祺硕
   in foreign exchange rates relating to receivables from and        ╯韰堑⹖㛛䆸ꉐ车㰇堑僗꟯╯㛛ⵜⵜ曎
   payables to foreign brokers and foreign currency deposits         ┘⮵闭Ⳃ䢦舜╯谱䮇겨ꤠ畚杼㷼泃㴕
   with banks. The management monitors foreign exchange              㛛ⵜ겨ꤠ┾亦䑒釒侇縓䜢㵴婵ꄆ㛻㛛
   exposures and will consider hedging significant foreign           ⵜ겨ꤠ
   exchange exposures should the need arise.

   It is the Group’s policy for each operating entity to operate     儗꥘㎯㶩⺨⠕祺揩㴨냇䫑⹦╯䷓瓁憡況
   in local currencies as far as possible to minimise currency       ⺎耉♓樧㏑鞁䆸祺揩㵩㛛䆸겨ꤠ僅㵸
   risks. Most of the Group’s principal businesses are conducted     ⴫儗꥘㎯╯㛻㛡丩╚釒哅ⳉ♓⩕⺨
   and recorded in Hong Kong dollar, the functional currency         舅꥘㎯㴨냇╯Ⱶ耉鞁䆸鶡车⹖⪌韰䘞
   of respective group entities, except for certain receivables      萵䇕䟕䷅⹖䟕♀㗟㛛祺硕╯韰堑⹖ꉐ车

    Market risk (Continued)                                          䄬㕙겨ꤠ箜
   Cash flow interest rate risk                                      杯ꄌ孲ꄈ⮵曎겨ꤠ
   The Group is exposed to cash flow interest rate risk in           儗꥘㎯ꪫ舃艃宰Ⳃ⮵曎䟕䷅韰堑ꉐ车
   relation to variable-rate accounts receivable, bank balances      磶궠♓⹖鞚堑⹖㗭堑僗꟯╯杯ꄌ孲ꄈ
   and loans and advances.                                           ⮵曎겨ꤠ

   As at 30 September 2025 and 30 September 2024, the                亦2025䇗9僖30仼⹖2024䇗9僖30仼
   Group’s cash flow interest rate risk is mainly related to the     儗꥘㎯╯杯ꄌ孲ꄈ⮵曎겨ꤠ╚釒艃儗꥘
   fluctuation of Prime Rate arising from the Group’s accounts       ㎯╯䟕䷅韰堑⹖鞚堑⹖㗭堑䢦榣榟╯僅
