39. Financial Risk Management Objectives and                        39. ꄌ踅겨ꤠ畚杼泘嘅⹖䷓瓁箜
    Policies (Continued)
    Credit risk and impairment assessment                              ⟔鞚겨ꤠ⹖峎⡭鍑⚽
   The Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk in the event            ⠭萵㵴䣆亡儔耉㷿车䏘璣╯鞊♴儗꥘
   of the counterparties’ failure to perform their obligations in      ㎯㶩⺨䄖澭鏂ꄌ踅鞳榣䢦ꪫ舃╯僅㛻
   relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the        ⟔鞚겨ꤠ憡禍⺭靸ⳉ敻媲辑䢦鬼╯鎊璣
   carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated       鞳榣╯韰ꪫ⡭憡㵩⟔鞚겨ꤠ僅㵸⴫儗
   statement of financial position. In order to minimise the           ꥘㎯畚杼㷼㡨孭┉䶺㎯꣣靷鞊稵䲶⟔
   credit risk, the management of the Group has a delegated            鞚⹖겨ꤠ畚杼䷓瓁♓䤊⬢⟔鞚꡿갯⹖
   team to compile the credit and risk management policies,            ꄊ㲋㵴鎊璣䦑埞䟕䷅韰堑䫑⹦♴⛰䷅韰
   to approve credit limits and to determine any debt recovery         车Ⳃ塝㛛儗꥘㎯亦㕕⼔僿儕囸鋁⺨⠕
   action on those delinquent receivables. In addition, the Group      ⮯䟕䷅韰堑╯⺎䷅㍑ꄌ갯♓澭⟊㶩扝
   reviews the recoverable amount of each individual receivable        嫏䷅㍑堑ꯁ⛼⭴餉㛭峎⡭谱䮇嶗⤔
   at the end of the reporting period to ensure that adequate
   impairment losses are made for irrecoverable amounts.

   The Group’s concentration of credit risk by geographical            儗꥘㎯䧞㏑㓊ⱁ⮇╯꥘╈⟔鞚겨ꤠ╚釒
   locations is mainly in Hong Kong. The Group has no                  ⛤亦꺞儗꥘㎯┾扝亦♴⛰ㄲ┉䟕䷅
   significant concentration of credit risk by any single debtor       韰堑ꄆ㛻꥘╈⟔鞚겨ꤠ塝╤榺亦겨ꤠ
   as the exposure is spread over a number of individual and           ⮇丆㛡⠕⠕☕⹖嚂啶㲙䢒䘞ꡭ錝21⹖
   institutional customers, except for the loans and advances          22䥑꨾╯鞚堑⹖㗭堑⹖䟕䷅韰堑ꢝ㛛
   and accounts receivable as disclosed in notes 21 and 22.

   The credit risks on bank balances are limited because the           榺亦㵴䣆亡憡榺㎜꤀⟔鞚鍑碍嚂啶鍑憡
   counterparties are financial institutions with high credit          냖⟔鞚鍑碍╯ꄌ踅嚂啶鎊ꉐ车磶궠╯
   ratings assigned by international credit-rating agencies.           ⟔鞚겨ꤠ僗꡿

   subject to ECL assessment based on qualitative and                 㲋ꧏ鶡车ꯏ僿⟔鞚谱䮇鍑⚽氳ꄌ踅鞳榣
   quantitative factors, if applicable, including overdue period      氳꣬夀鎊璣㍕碜ⴎ䦮⮵䕜⹖儗ꄌ鷀僿
   of interest and principal, change in fair value of pledged         僿ꝴ⮇⮯銕氪鴓ⱶ⟊镜ꄌ鵡湳釒姲䬠
   listed securities or properties that triggered the margin calls    ❠갯㛛䥡䥨⿁䡝啶䡗鸂硝◄♭╯䄖䥡䥨
   for additional collateral or constituted events of breach of       ┕䄬镜⯉䡝擻哅╯⪝䇖⡭闭Ⳃ♓⹖鴓ⱶ
   contracts respectively, and status of margin calls.                ⟊镜ꄌ鵡湳氳敻䛟

   Information that will be taken into account when assessing         鍑⚽⟔鞚겨ꤠ㛻䆋㘎ⱶ侇㵩縓䜢╯鞳乢
   significant increase in credit risk are also set out in note 3.    ◱鬼⮛亦ꡭ錝3

         professional valuer not connected to the Group. The fair value                  ⡭ 縡 鷾 舜⪝ 䇖 ⡭ ╤ 㓹 亦 䄬 㕙 嫏鵡 鷪 奄
         was based on the market approach, by comparing recent market                    鬴鲥僿龫鲥㏑ⶆ⛍垷沠氳䄬㕙◬佄ꤛ
         transactions of similar interests located in the surrounding area               䏳㓹亦奄鬴鼨⮇㵴⺎鞳奄鬴◬佄氳◬佄
         and then making adjustments to the transaction prices of the                    ⧎劲⛼⭴鐪丞縡ꄊ㲋亦ꄊ㲋注꟯擻哅╯
         comparables based on elements of comparison. In determining                     ⪝䇖⡭侇畚杼㷼艃⺭鞳劲㛛鼨⚽⡭䅨秵
         the fair value of the relevant properties, the management work                  㳢⺭⛼♓㶩⪝䇖⡭銷ꄈꄊ㲋鸸樧氳⚽⡭
         closely with the qualified external valuer to determine appropriate             䤗 轱 ⹖ 鮗 ⪌ 丩 䴆畚 杼 㷼 鏂 憡鎊 璣 擻 哅
         valuation techniques and inputs for fair value measurements. The                ⪝ 䇖 ⡭ ╯ ╚ 釒 鐪 丞 艃 ꟯ 亦 ꪫ 犉俍 鈶⛤
         management considered that the major adjustment on the fair                     糍⹖注㵴韭碜╯鐪丞僗꟯
         value of these properties related to the adjustment relating to size,
         view, location and relative quality.

   Liquidity risk                                                                孲Ⳃ鞳ꄌ겨ꤠ
   Internally generated cash flow, bank borrowings are the                       ⪐鼨榣榟杯ꄌ孲ꄈꉐ车⠺堑憡䲡♀儗
   sources of funds to finance the operations of the Group.                      ꥘㎯祺揩䢦ꧏ╯鞳ꄌ✿嶏儗꥘㎯╯㛻
   The majority of the Group’s banking facilities are subject                    㛡丩ꉐ车踅鞳憡⹨宰Ⳃ⮵曎䢦釨꡿┾亦
   to floating rate and are renewable annually. The Group’s                      夷䇗◀♓ꄆ箜儗꥘㎯╯孲Ⳃ鞳ꄌ겨ꤠ
   liquidity risk management includes making available standby                   畚杼ⴎ䦮⹦䐃⺎Ⳃ榫╯⤔榫ꉐ车踅鞳⹖
   banking facilities and diversifying the funding sources. The                  㵩鞳ꄌ✿嶏㛡嘚⴫儗꥘㎯㲋僿囸鋁╚
   Group regularly reviews the major funding positions to ensure                 釒鞳ꄌ敻媲♓澭⟊⺎暢䐃餉㛭靸䷓鞳
   adequate financial resources are available to meet their                      嶏♓䟕♀䏘璣⺨舅╯靸ⳉ鞊♴
   respective financial obligations.

       of a traded price is not possible, the Group will seek          姲⪭☾䄬㕙鞳乢♓꿨镜注꟯ꄌ

       of relevant financial asset or financial liabilities, with      ⮯蜸ꄆ逈釲憡鬴僗꟯鵿⹖鬴憡⺎ꪥ
       greater weight given to information that is considered          氳鞳乢
       to be more relevant and reliable.
